> Examine this goose program, and evaluate it for several things: Reliability,  
  consistency, and feature parity across platforms and architectures. We're     
  focused on Windows and Linux primarily, and Mac OS as well. Preferably, if we 
  can build this for Mac OS natively as well, that'd be fantastic.              
                                                                                
  The entire goal is to launch on all three platforms (Windows, Linux, and Mac  
  OS), and for ARM, x64, and x86. Everything should be planned out already, but 
  this was built entirely by Codex 5.5, and I'm going to have Fable drive       
  this. I trust your intelligence, world knowledge, and coding ability a little 
  bit more.                                                                     
                                                                                
  Go through this entire thing, examine all of it, and evaluate all of it. We   
  want to ensure that this is functioning as intended. I also want you to see   
  if you can redo the rest, or at least redo how it's rendering currently.      
  Maybe rewrite the render, but I need it to work cross-platform.               
                                                                                
  The goose right now looks not great, like a few SVGs hobbled together, and    
  it's not animated very well. We've got all the basics there, but it just      
  doesn't look great right now. Your goal is to make the goose look better and  
  function as it's supposed to across all platforms, and to make sure the       
  program functions correctly in and of itself.                                 
                                                                                
  Installing and uninstalling should be smooth, and all the terminal commands

  configuration there, like all the options, so you know how it should go. Ask  
  questions if you need further clarification on anything. Use

  ⎿  · You said ARM, x64, and x86. macOS hasn't supported 32-bit apps since

● User answered Claude's questions:
  ⎿  · The goose's look: Codex's recorded decision (ADR 0001, board task #r2v) 
     was to replace the procedural renderer with a pre-baked CPU sprite/atlas 
     blitter. Having read the code, I disagree: the tiny-skia vector pipeline is
     sound and cheap — what's crude is the rig assembly + animation (feet slide
     because the original's plant-and-swing gait was dropped for a sine wave; 
     straight-capsule neck; no easing/blink/secondary motion). A sprite atlas 
     would also conflict with the plan's "no sprite art" guardrail and 
     complicate your live RGB palette editing. I recommend superseding it with 
     "Procedural Vector V2": keep tiny-skia, rewrite the rig + animation 
     (planted feet, bezier S-curve neck, refined silhouette with tail, easing, 
     blink/idle motion). Which direction? → Procedural Vector V2 (Recommended)
     · The website repo has significant UNCOMMITTED work on main (src/main.jsx 
     +215/−41, styles.css +25): the entire live GitHub-release fetching layer 
     that powers the deployed look. I didn't author it (Codex did) but it's 
     finished, verified accurate, and a redeploy from HEAD today would regress 
     the site. How should the plan treat it? → Commit it as-is first 
     (Recommended)

● These references land perfectly — and they raise the bar in a well-defined
  way. What I'm taking from them:

  - Flat-illustration quality: layered slate-grey wing with scalloped feather
  notches, two-tone beak (nostril + darker underside), subtle body shading
  (throat/underbody/behind-wing), separate orange legs with ankle bend + webbed
  feet in two tones, tail tip — not the current "compact blob."
  - The top-down view is a big hint: a detailed side-profile goose can't just
  rotate 90° when walking vertically like the current renderer does. I'll design
  a dual-view rig — side profile (mirrored left/right) for shallow headings and
  idle/interactions, top-down for steep headings, with a quick crossfade at the
  threshold. The top-down rig rotates cleanly to any angle.
  - Palette expansion: the art needs ~6 tones (body, shade, wing, orange, dark
  orange, outline) instead of today's 3 — extending [colors] config + TUI rows
  back-compatibly, with new defaults matching this softer reference look.
  - Since these are your own generated SVGs (not original-app assets), I can
  adapt their actual path data as the starting geometry for the parametric parts
  — no clean-room concern.

  Updating the plan and briefing the Plan agent with the raised art direction:

  Searched for 1 pattern (ctrl+o to expand)
● Confirmed — all four are clean Quiver AI vector SVGs (8–25 paths each, cubic
  bezier data) with exactly the ~6-tone palette I proposed: body #EDEDED, shade
  #C6C6C6, wing #515557, orange #FC7927, dark orange #D1551B. The geometry can
  be decomposed into parametric rig-driven parts directly.

  One art note for implementation: the reference style is outline-less flat
  design (contrast comes from the shade tone), but a desktop pet needs edge
  definition against arbitrary backgrounds — I'll keep a thin configurable
  outline defaulting to something subtle like the shade tone, and the existing
  soft ground shadow.

  The Plan agent is finishing the staged implementation design with this art
  direction folded in — I'll assemble the final plan when it lands.

     │                                                                         │
     │ A. Engine + renderer (agent 1 + firsthand read of render.rs/rig.rs)     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Tech stack is sound; the artistry/animation is the problem.             │
     │ - Renderer: crates/honk-engine/src/render.rs — tiny-skia CPU vector     │
     │ rasterization, anti-aliased, back-to-front painter: stipple shadow →    │
     │ neck capsule → body (closed cubic-bezier path in (fwd,across) local     │
     │ space) → head capsule → feet (flat 5-point polygons) → beak (bezier     │
     │ wedge) → eye (disc + highlight). Palette-driven (M15 RGB editing        │
     │ preserved). render_rig_with_palette at render.rs:342.                   │
     │ - Rig: rig.rs::Rig::update(center, direction_deg, neck_lerp,            │
     │ gait_phase) — verified Exports.cs constants asserted in tests           │
     │ (rig.rs:162), but ~9 of them (UnderBody*, HeadRadius1, IPD,             │
     │ NeccHeight2…) are asserted yet unused by the draw code;                 │
     │ underbody_center computed but never drawn (dead geometry).              │
     │ - Animation weaknesses (why it "looks like hobbled SVGs"):              │
     │   a. Feet are stateless sine (feet.rs::gait) — the original C#          │
     │ ProceduralFeets is a stateful plant-and-swing with overshoot (foot      │
     │ stays planted, then swings ahead). Current feet slide/moonwalk. Biggest │
     │ fidelity gap.                                                           │
     │   b. Neck is one straight capsule neck_base→neck_head — no S-curve, no  │
     │ head-kept-level articulation.                                           │
     │   c. Whole-body rotation with direction + minimal secondary motion:     │
     │ body bob is abs(sin)*1.5 only; no head bob, no breathing idle, no       │
     │ blink, no tail geometry.                                                │
     │   d. neck_lerp applied linearly (no easing).                            │
     │ - Golden tests: tests/golden.rs +                                       │
     │ tests/golden/{rest,reaching,mid_stride}.png are self-generated — any    │
     │ renderer rewrite re-blesses them (they pin determinism, not art         │
     │ quality).                                                               │
     │ - Renderer V2 pre-decision: board #r2v + ADR 0001 chose a "CPU          │
     │ sprite/atlas blitter" — but that conflicts with plan §5.2 "no sprite    │
     │ art" guardrail (honk300_plan.md:281-285) and would sacrifice live       │
     │ palette editing + resolution independence. Recommendation: supersede    │
     │ with "Procedural Vector V2" — keep tiny-skia, rewrite rig assembly +    │
     │ animation (planted feet, bezier neck, refined silhouette w/ tail,       │
     │ easing, blink/idle motion). New ADR.                                    │
     │ - Deck RNG: faithful-biased port is the locked decision (C8), pinned by │
     │ test.                                                                   │
     │ - Extra agent-1 findings: mood-driven neck posture snaps un-damped      │
     │ (world.rs:835-849); ADR 0001:57-63 is where the sprite/atlas V2         │
     │ direction lives (to supersede). Nuance: C# ProceduralFeets public API   │
     │ exposes only fields + 3 constants (Exports.cs:316-326) — the            │
     │ plant/swing update math is in the closed binary, so the V2 gait is a    │
     │ clean-room stateful reconstruction (plant → swing with 0.4 overshoot    │
     │ when lag > 5px) guided by those constants, not a literal port.          │
     │                                                                         │

     │ │ Install/Uninstall/ │           │ MISS (returns │         │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ Update             │ Impl      │  Err —        │ Impl    │ Impl       │

     │ └────────────────────┴───────────┴───────────────┴─────────┴─────────── │
     │ ──┘                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Ranked reliability concerns:                                            │
     │ 1. Windows collect-window blocks the single render/sim thread up to ~3s │
     │ (notepad spawn+poll, honk-platform-windows/lib.rs:458-466,649-668) —    │
     │ sim freezes AND IPC times out (2s RESPONSE_TIMEOUT). Windows-only       │
     │ freeze.                                                                 │
     │ 2. No Windows DPI awareness — no manifest/PMv2/WM_DPICHANGED (plan      │
     │ mandates it, honk300_plan.md:772). Mixed-DPI → mispositioned/blurry     │
     │ overlay, cursor skew. Largest Windows correctness gap.                  │
     │ 3. Notepad process/window leak — ControlledWindow::Notepad has no       │
     │ Drop/DestroyWindow (lib.rs:543-545,589-595); collected Notepads survive │
     │ stop.                                                                   │
     │ 4. Unix stale singleton lock — create_new lockfile, no PID/flock        │
     │ liveness check (honk-control/platform.rs:335-368); crash ⇒ false        │
     │ "already running" on macOS/Linux. Windows named mutex unaffected.       │
     │ 5. macOS Overlay::pump is bare updateWindows() — no NSApp event drain   │
     │ (lib.rs:215-218); collect-window close buttons unserviced.              │
     │ 6. Linux silent headless zombie — overlay-creation failure silently     │
     │ falls back to no-op HeadlessOverlay (lib.rs:149-173); status says       │
     │ "running", nothing renders, eprintln discarded when detached.           │
     │ 7. IPC single-client, blocking; slow sim ⇒ all control commands         │
     │ TIMEOUT.                                                                │
     │ 8. macOS bitmap over reused external Vec plane ptr (lib.rs:419-452) —   │
     │ AppKit doesn't copy; tearing/UAF risk.                                  │
     │ 9. Linux X11 per-frame XFixes region alloc/destroy + event-mask re-set  │
     │ churn.                                                                  │
     │ 10. Pervasive swallowed failures (let _ = + one-shot eprintln invisible │
     │ when detached).                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Non-issues: hot loops avoid unwrap/expect; CommandServer joined on      │
     │ Drop. macOS code is REAL (objc2 AppKit, universal2 .app CI-proven for   │
     │ permissionless surface); Accessibility-granted mischief evidence        │
     │ pending a pre-granted Mac (#m16r). macOS presence hard-unsupported.     │
     │ Linux session detection: X11 whenever DISPLAY exists; --wayland opt-in; │
     │ collect unsupported on all Linux.                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ C. Packaging / config / control / TUI (agent 3 tail notes + firsthand   │
     │ reads)                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Built targets today (cross-verified by agent 4 against real v0.1.0      │
     │ release assets):                                                        │
     │ - cargo-dist (release.yml, generated v0.31, driven by                   │
     │ [workspace.metadata.dist].targets in root Cargo.toml):                  │
     │ x86_64/aarch64-pc-windows-msvc ZIPs,                                    │
     │ x86_64/aarch64-unknown-linux-{gnu,musl} tar.xz, honk300-installer.sh +  │
     │ honk300-installer.ps1.                                                  │
     │ - Hand-authored windows-installers.yml: Global/Corporate MSI (wix/,     │
     │ wix-corporate/) + EXE (inno/) for x64+ARM64, all with .sha256 sidecars. │
     │ 14 Windows+Linux assets total; zero macOS assets                        │
     │ (script/package_macos_app.sh exists for local staging only).            │
     │ - Adding macOS = add 2 apple triples to dist targets (tar.xz +          │
     │ shell-installer pickup ~free) + hand-authored macos job building        │
     │ universal2 .app + DMG (hdiutil) + sha256, unsigned personal-use.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Lifecycle gaps:                                                         │
     │ - macOS install/uninstall stubbed → Err                                 │
     │ (src/install.rs:134-137,194-197); update hard-errors on macOS           │
     │ (update.rs:150-156). Windows/Linux implemented (LOCALAPPDATA/XDG, PATH, │
     │ Start Menu/desktop+autostart, 3 name aliases).                          │
     │ - Non-purge uninstall deletes user memes/notes without backup           │
     │ (install.rs:158,183) — data-loss footgun.                               │
     │ - update blocked on 32-bit hosts (fine — we're x64/ARM64 only).         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Config/TUI gaps: behavior.attack_randomly persisted but never mapped in │
     │ effective_options (dead key — it's an original-parity feature           │
     │ [spontaneous cursor attacks], so WIRE it into the roam deck gated       │
     │ default-off rather than delete); mouse.grab_distance/drop_distance      │
     │ engine-wired but not TUI-editable.                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Config schema (firsthand, honk-config/src/lib.rs:24-370): 15 sections — │
     │ behavior (silence_sounds, can_attack_mouse, attack_randomly,            │
     │ use_custom_colors, first/min/max_wandering_time_seconds), colors        │
     │ (goose_white/orange/outline hex), speeds (walk/run/charge_speed,        │
     │ acceleration_normal/charged, step_time_normal/charged, stop_radius),    │
     │ mud (3 timings), mouse (grab/drop_distance, succ_time), behaviors       │
     │ (on_hour_double_honk, multi_monitor_chase), moods (dynamic_moods,       │
     │ mood_intensity), mischief (perch_and_ride,                              │
     │ collect_windows/notes/memes), interaction (pat_streak), schedule        │
     │ (quiet_hours_enabled, quiet_start/end, dnd_respect, seasonal, autumn),  │
     │ appearance (calm_goose), audio (enabled, honk, bite, mud, pat), safety  │
     │ (pause_on_fullscreen, no_mouse_steal, no_window_ride), platform         │
     │ (wayland). Versioned (goose_config_version), unknown-key-tolerant.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ IPC: named pipe (Win) / Unix socket; single-client, 2s                  │
     │ RESPONSE_TIMEOUT; Unix singleton = create_new lockfile, no liveness     │
     │ check (stale-lock false positive after crash —                          │
     │ honk-control/platform.rs:335-368).                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Spicy behaviors doc                                                     │

     │ R1 — Reliability + correctness fixes (gates everything; DPI must        │
     │ precede R2's visual verification)                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Windows PMv2 DPI awareness —                                         │
     │ SetProcessDpiAwarenessContext(PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2) at startup before   │
     │ any HWND (simplest correct mechanism; embedded manifest optional        │
     │ later); handle WM_DPICHANGED/WM_DISPLAYCHANGE by re-enumerating         │
     │ monitors + rebuilding overlay windows. Files:                           │
     │ crates/honk-platform-windows/src/lib.rs, src/runtime/windows.rs.        │
     │ 2. Non-blocking collect-window — replace the ≤3s spawn/poll + 60ms      │
     │ sleeps with a per-tick polled state machine (deadline-based); track the │
     │ Notepad child (process handle), WM_CLOSE→terminate fallback on          │
     │ close/stop; Drop for ControlledWindow::Notepad. Fixes sim freeze + IPC  │
     │ TIMEOUT + zombie leak. Files: honk-platform-windows/lib.rs              │
     │ (CollectWindowController), runtime/windows.rs.                          │
     │ 3. Unix singleton liveness — replace create_new lockfile with advisory  │
     │ flock (auto-released on process death; rustix unix-only dep in          │
     │ honk-control). Files: honk-control/src/platform.rs, its Cargo.toml.     │
     │ 4. macOS pump + present safety — drain NSApp events                     │
     │ (nextEventMatchingMask loop) instead of bare updateWindows(); make      │
     │ NSBitmapImageRep own its pixel buffer (copy-in) instead of wrapping the │
     │ reused Vec pointer. Files: honk-platform-macos/src/lib.rs.              │
     │ 5. Linux degraded-overlay honesty — HeadlessOverlay fallback only under │
     │ explicit HONK300_ALLOW_HEADLESS=1 (CI smoke); otherwise fail start      │
     │ loudly. Add an overlay capability to the status protocol so             │
     │ degraded/failed render states show in status + TUI Status tab. Files:   │
     │ honk-platform-linux/lib.rs, runtime/linux.rs, honk-control/protocol.rs, │
     │ TUI status. (+cheap X11 nits: cache the XFixes input region, re-set     │
     │ only on bbox change; set event mask once.)                              │
     │ 6. Config/TUI/lifecycle gaps — wire behavior.attack_randomly into the   │
     │ roam deck (spontaneous nab, gated on can_attack_mouse + warp            │
     │ capability, default off — original-parity); TUI rows for                │
     │ mouse.grab_distance/drop_distance; non-purge uninstall must leave user  │
     │ memes/notes untouched (purge keeps existing backup semantics). Files:   │
     │ honk-engine (world/task), honk-config, honk-config-tui, src/install.rs. │
     │ - ADR 0015 (reliability/platform-safety contract). Sync                 │
     │ README/AGENTS/CLAUDE/honk300_plan where behavior changes.               │
     │ - Verify: local gate; Windows smoke — collect note/meme while status    │
     │ stays responsive (no TIMEOUT), stop leaves no Notepad zombie, DPI       │
     │ context assertion + visual position check; WSL/WSLg — kill -9 then      │
     │ restart (no stale-lock false positive), headless fallback errors loudly │
     │ without the env var; cargo check all 8 apple/linux/windows triples; CI  │
     │ smoke scripts green.                                                    │

● Updated plan
  ⎿  /plan to preview
  ⎿  User rejected Claude's plan:
     ╭─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
     │ honk300 Full Evaluation + Renderer Rewrite + Cross-Platform Launch Plan │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ▎ STATUS: FINAL. Evaluation complete (4 Explore agents + firsthand      │
     │ ▎ reads + 1 Plan agent). Verdict up front: the program is               │
     │ ▎ architecturally sound and honestly built — platform-free engine, real │
     │ ▎ macOS backend, honest capability degradation, accurate website. The   │
     │ ▎ gaps are: a crude renderer/animator (the marquee fix), ~10 concrete   │
     │ ▎ reliability defects, missing macOS packaging/lifecycle, and small     │
     │ ▎ config/TUI/site gaps. Plan = 4 execution rounds + a deferred bucket.  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Context                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ honk300 is a from-scratch Rust reimplementation of Desktop Goose        │
     │ (M0–M19 implemented by Codex 5.5, v0.1.0 released). Emmett is handing   │
     │ the driver's seat to Fable and wants:                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Full evaluation — reliability, consistency, feature parity across    │
     │ Windows / Linux / macOS.                                                │
     │ 2. Renderer rework — the goose "looks like a few SVGs hobbled together, │
     │ not animated very well." Make it look better, cross-platform.           │
     │ 3. Program correctness — everything functions as intended:              │
     │ install/uninstall smooth, terminal commands                             │
     │ (start/stop/reload/do/status) work, the TUI works, config options all   │
     │ present.                                                                │
     │ 4. Launch on all three platforms — Windows, Linux, macOS.               │
     │                                                                         │

     │ Known repo facts                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Workspace: honk-engine (platform-free, forbid(unsafe)), honk-control  │
     │ (IPC), honk-config, honk-config-tui (ratatui),                          │
     │ honk-platform-{windows,macos,linux}, root honk300 bin.                  │
     │ - Renderer today: procedural rig → tiny_skia::Pixmap (plan §7 render.rs │
     │ # Rig -> tiny_skia::Pixmap, dirty-rect aware), golden-frame tests.      │
     │ - 120 Hz sim; present on-dirty rate-capped; one overlay HWND per        │
     │ monitor (Windows).                                                      │
     │ - Local gate: fmt --check, clippy -D warnings, test --workspace, build  │
     │ --release. Rust 1.95, edition 2021.                                     │
     │ - Changelog rule: CHANGELOG.md + HUMAN_CHANGELOG.md in lockstep, same   │
     │ commit.                                                                 │

     │ (no tray).                                                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Findings (to fill from exploration)                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A. Engine + renderer (agent 1 + firsthand read of render.rs/rig.rs)     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Tech stack is sound; the artistry/animation is the problem.             │
     │ - Renderer: crates/honk-engine/src/render.rs — tiny-skia CPU vector     │
     │ rasterization, anti-aliased, back-to-front painter: stipple shadow →    │
     │ neck capsule → body (closed cubic-bezier path in (fwd,across) local     │
     │ space) → head capsule → feet (flat 5-point polygons) → beak (bezier     │
     │ wedge) → eye (disc + highlight). Palette-driven (M15 RGB editing        │
     │ preserved). render_rig_with_palette at render.rs:342.                   │
     │ - Rig: rig.rs::Rig::update(center, direction_deg, neck_lerp,            │
     │ gait_phase) — verified Exports.cs constants asserted in tests           │
     │ (rig.rs:162), but ~9 of them (UnderBody*, HeadRadius1, IPD,             │
     │ NeccHeight2…) are asserted yet unused by the draw code;                 │
     │ underbody_center computed but never drawn (dead geometry).              │
     │ - Animation weaknesses (why it "looks like hobbled SVGs"):              │
     │   a. Feet are stateless sine (feet.rs::gait) — the original C#          │
     │ ProceduralFeets is a stateful plant-and-swing with overshoot (foot      │
     │ stays planted, then swings ahead). Current feet slide/moonwalk. Biggest │
     │ fidelity gap.                                                           │
     │   b. Neck is one straight capsule neck_base→neck_head — no S-curve, no  │
     │ head-kept-level articulation.                                           │
     │   c. Whole-body rotation with direction + minimal secondary motion:     │
     │ body bob is abs(sin)*1.5 only; no head bob, no breathing idle, no       │
     │ blink, no tail geometry.                                                │
     │   d. neck_lerp applied linearly (no easing).                            │
     │ - Golden tests: tests/golden.rs +                                       │
     │ tests/golden/{rest,reaching,mid_stride}.png are self-generated — any    │
     │ renderer rewrite re-blesses them (they pin determinism, not art         │
     │ quality).                                                               │
     │ - Renderer V2 pre-decision: board #r2v + ADR 0001 chose a "CPU          │
     │ sprite/atlas blitter" — but that conflicts with plan §5.2 "no sprite    │
     │ art" guardrail (honk300_plan.md:281-285) and would sacrifice live       │
     │ palette editing + resolution independence. Recommendation: supersede    │
     │ with "Procedural Vector V2" — keep tiny-skia, rewrite rig assembly +    │
     │ animation (planted feet, bezier neck, refined silhouette w/ tail,       │
     │ easing, blink/idle motion). New ADR.                                    │
     │ - Deck RNG: faithful-biased port is the locked decision (C8), pinned by │
     │ test.                                                                   │
     │ - Extra agent-1 findings: mood-driven neck posture snaps un-damped      │
     │ (world.rs:835-849); ADR 0001:57-63 is where the sprite/atlas V2         │
     │ direction lives (to supersede). Nuance: C# ProceduralFeets public API   │
     │ exposes only fields + 3 constants (Exports.cs:316-326) — the            │
     │ plant/swing update math is in the closed binary, so the V2 gait is a    │
     │ clean-room stateful reconstruction (plant → swing with 0.4 overshoot    │
     │ when lag > 5px) guided by those constants, not a literal port.          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ART DIRECTION (locked by Emmett, 2026-07-07 — reference images          │
     │ supplied):                                                              │
     │ Reference SVGs/PNGs in C:\Users\hey\Downloads\gooooose_sprite_examples\ │
     │ + C:\Users\hey\Downloads\a_flat_goose_sprite_019f02b5-6d09-7a9b-b3b4-1  │
     │ bcf3b5fbdc3.svg (Emmett's own generated art — safe to adapt path data   │
     │ directly; copy into docs/art-reference/ during implementation). Target  │
     │ = flat-illustration quality, "doesn't need to be exact, but that level  │
     │ of effort":                                                             │
     │ 1. Layered dark-slate wing with scalloped feather notches (new palette  │
     │ slot).                                                                  │
     │ 2. Two-tone beak: orange + darker-orange underside/shading + nostril    │
     │ mark.                                                                   │
     │ 3. Subtle body shading: throat/neck-front shade, underbody shade,       │
     │ behind-wing highlight (light-grey shade tone).                          │
     │ 4. Separate orange legs (shin + ankle bend) with webbed feet, two-tone. │
     │ 5. Tail tip with shade; long tapered S-neck widening into shoulders.    │
     │ 6. Dual-view rig: side profile (h-mirrored) for shallow headings +      │
     │ idle/interactions; top-down view (supplied as reference #2) for steep   │
     │ headings, rotating freely to heading; quick crossfade (~100-150ms) at   │
     │ threshold (~±50° from horizontal). Replaces the current                 │
     │ full-rotation-of-side-profile behavior that looks wrong walking         │
     │ up/down.                                                                │
     │ 7. Palette: 3 → ~6 configurable tones (goose_white, goose_shade,        │
     │ goose_wing, goose_orange, goose_orange_dark, goose_outline) — [colors]  │
     │ config + TUI RGB rows extended back-compatibly; new softer defaults     │
     │ matching the reference (off-white body, slate wing).                    │
     │ 8. Detail must read at desktop size (~60-90px tall) — consider 2x       │
     │ supersampled goose raster downscaled on present.                        │

     │ B. Platform parity + reliability (agent 2 — full report received)       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Architecture: No shared Rust trait — capability contract is duck-typed  │
     │ same-named modules; the three runtime loops                             │
     │ (src/runtime/{windows,macos,linux}.rs) are hand-cloned copies.          │
     │ Capability flows:                                                       │
     │ BackendCapability{Supported,Unsupported,Denied,Failed} → collapsed to   │
     │ bools in WorldOptions; user-facing CapabilityStatus over IPC.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Parity matrix (Impl/Deg/Perm/N-A/Miss):                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌────────────────────┬───────────┬───────────────┬─────────┬─────────── │
     │ ──┐                                                                     │
     │ │      Feature       │    Win    │     macOS     │   X11   │   Wayland  │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │                    │ Impl (1   │ Impl (NSWindo │ Impl    │ Deg        │
     │ (single │                                                               │
     │ │ Overlay render     │ win/monit │ w/display)    │ (ARGB   │  1280×720  │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                    │ or)       │               │ dock)   │ layer)     │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │                    │           │               │ Deg     │ N/A        │
     │ (click- │                                                               │
     │ │ Per-pixel hit test │ Impl      │ Deg (bbox)    │ (XShape │ through)   │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                    │           │               │  bbox)  │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ Multi-monitor      │ Impl      │ Impl          │ Impl    │ N/A        │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ Cursor nab         │ Impl      │ Perm (AX)     │ Impl    │ N/A        │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ Window ride        │ Impl      │ Perm (AX)     │ Impl    │ N/A        │
     │  │                                                                      │

     │ │ Collect windows    │ Impl      │ Impl/Perm     │ N/A     │ N/A        │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │                    │ Impl      │               │ ffplay/ │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ Sound              │ rodio     │ afplay shell  │ mpv     │ same       │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                    │           │               │ shell   │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ Presence           │ Impl      │ N/A           │ N/A     │ N/A        │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ (DND/fullscreen)   │           │               │         │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ DPI awareness      │ MISS      │ Impl (points) │ n/a     │ n/a        │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ IPC / TUI          │ Impl      │ Impl          │ Impl    │ Impl       │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ Install/Uninstall/ │           │ MISS (returns │         │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ Update             │ Impl      │  Err —        │ Impl    │ Impl       │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                    │           │ deferred)     │         │            │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ └────────────────────┴───────────┴───────────────┴─────────┴─────────── │
     │ ──┘                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Ranked reliability concerns:                                            │
     │ 1. Windows collect-window blocks the single render/sim thread up to ~3s │
     │ (notepad spawn+poll, honk-platform-windows/lib.rs:458-466,649-668) —    │
     │ sim freezes AND IPC times out (2s RESPONSE_TIMEOUT). Windows-only       │
     │ freeze.                                                                 │
     │ 2. No Windows DPI awareness — no manifest/PMv2/WM_DPICHANGED (plan      │
     │ mandates it, honk300_plan.md:772). Mixed-DPI → mispositioned/blurry     │
     │ overlay, cursor skew. Largest Windows correctness gap.                  │
     │ 3. Notepad process/window leak — ControlledWindow::Notepad has no       │
     │ Drop/DestroyWindow (lib.rs:543-545,589-595); collected Notepads survive │
     │ stop.                                                                   │
     │ 4. Unix stale singleton lock — create_new lockfile, no PID/flock        │
     │ liveness check (honk-control/platform.rs:335-368); crash ⇒ false        │
     │ "already running" on macOS/Linux. Windows named mutex unaffected.       │
     │ 5. macOS Overlay::pump is bare updateWindows() — no NSApp event drain   │
     │ (lib.rs:215-218); collect-window close buttons unserviced.              │
     │ 6. Linux silent headless zombie — overlay-creation failure silently     │
     │ falls back to no-op HeadlessOverlay (lib.rs:149-173); status says       │
     │ "running", nothing renders, eprintln discarded when detached.           │
     │ 7. IPC single-client, blocking; slow sim ⇒ all control commands         │
     │ TIMEOUT.                                                                │
     │ 8. macOS bitmap over reused external Vec plane ptr (lib.rs:419-452) —   │
     │ AppKit doesn't copy; tearing/UAF risk.                                  │
     │ 9. Linux X11 per-frame XFixes region alloc/destroy + event-mask re-set  │
     │ churn.                                                                  │
     │ 10. Pervasive swallowed failures (let _ = + one-shot eprintln invisible │
     │ when detached).                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Non-issues: hot loops avoid unwrap/expect; CommandServer joined on      │
     │ Drop. macOS code is REAL (objc2 AppKit, universal2 .app CI-proven for   │
     │ permissionless surface); Accessibility-granted mischief evidence        │
     │ pending a pre-granted Mac (#m16r). macOS presence hard-unsupported.     │
     │ Linux session detection: X11 whenever DISPLAY exists; --wayland opt-in; │
     │ collect unsupported on all Linux.                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ C. Packaging / config / control / TUI (agent 3 tail notes + firsthand   │
     │ reads)                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Built targets today (cross-verified by agent 4 against real v0.1.0      │
     │ release assets):                                                        │
     │ - cargo-dist (release.yml, generated v0.31, driven by                   │
     │ [workspace.metadata.dist].targets in root Cargo.toml):                  │
     │ x86_64/aarch64-pc-windows-msvc ZIPs,                                    │
     │ x86_64/aarch64-unknown-linux-{gnu,musl} tar.xz, honk300-installer.sh +  │
     │ honk300-installer.ps1.                                                  │
     │ - Hand-authored windows-installers.yml: Global/Corporate MSI (wix/,     │
     │ wix-corporate/) + EXE (inno/) for x64+ARM64, all with .sha256 sidecars. │
     │ 14 Windows+Linux assets total; zero macOS assets                        │
     │ (script/package_macos_app.sh exists for local staging only).            │
     │ - Adding macOS = add 2 apple triples to dist targets (tar.xz +          │
     │ shell-installer pickup ~free) + hand-authored macos job building        │
     │ universal2 .app + DMG (hdiutil) + sha256, unsigned personal-use.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Lifecycle gaps:                                                         │
     │ - macOS install/uninstall stubbed → Err                                 │
     │ (src/install.rs:134-137,194-197); update hard-errors on macOS           │
     │ (update.rs:150-156). Windows/Linux implemented (LOCALAPPDATA/XDG, PATH, │
     │ Start Menu/desktop+autostart, 3 name aliases).                          │
     │ - Non-purge uninstall deletes user memes/notes without backup           │
     │ (install.rs:158,183) — data-loss footgun.                               │
     │ - update blocked on 32-bit hosts (fine — we're x64/ARM64 only).         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Config/TUI gaps: behavior.attack_randomly persisted but never mapped in │
     │ effective_options (dead key — it's an original-parity feature           │
     │ [spontaneous cursor attacks], so WIRE it into the roam deck gated       │
     │ default-off rather than delete); mouse.grab_distance/drop_distance      │
     │ engine-wired but not TUI-editable.                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Config schema (firsthand, honk-config/src/lib.rs:24-370): 15 sections — │
     │ behavior (silence_sounds, can_attack_mouse, attack_randomly,            │
     │ use_custom_colors, first/min/max_wandering_time_seconds), colors        │
     │ (goose_white/orange/outline hex), speeds (walk/run/charge_speed,        │
     │ acceleration_normal/charged, step_time_normal/charged, stop_radius),    │
     │ mud (3 timings), mouse (grab/drop_distance, succ_time), behaviors       │
     │ (on_hour_double_honk, multi_monitor_chase), moods (dynamic_moods,       │
     │ mood_intensity), mischief (perch_and_ride,                              │
     │ collect_windows/notes/memes), interaction (pat_streak), schedule        │
     │ (quiet_hours_enabled, quiet_start/end, dnd_respect, seasonal, autumn),  │
     │ appearance (calm_goose), audio (enabled, honk, bite, mud, pat), safety  │
     │ (pause_on_fullscreen, no_mouse_steal, no_window_ride), platform         │
     │ (wayland). Versioned (goose_config_version), unknown-key-tolerant.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ IPC: named pipe (Win) / Unix socket; single-client, 2s                  │
     │ RESPONSE_TIMEOUT; Unix singleton = create_new lockfile, no liveness     │
     │ check (stale-lock false positive after crash —                          │
     │ honk-control/platform.rs:335-368).                                      │

     │ The Plan — execution rounds                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Each round: branch off main, keep .tasks/TASKS.md + task files current, │
     │ CHANGELOG.md + HUMAN_CHANGELOG.md in lockstep, local gate (fmt --check  │
     │ / clippy -D warnings / test --workspace / build --release), pause for   │
     │ Emmett's go-ahead before committing (his standing preference). Windows  │
     │ GUI changes get visual verification with screenshots before claiming    │
     │ done.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ R0 — Setup (tiny)                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - /tasks-start resume ritual; restructure board: new tasks for R1–R4    │
     │ below; supersede #r2v, fold #c0d into the Renderer V2 task.             │
     │ - Copy the 4 reference goose SVGs from Downloads into                   │
     │ docs/art-reference/ (Emmett's own Quiver AI art — safe to adapt).       │
     │ - Commit the untracked                                                  │
     │ docs/thinking/2026-07-07-b9e-spicy-behaviors-plan.md as the #b9e        │
     │ handoff (out of scope otherwise).                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ R1 — Reliability + correctness fixes (gates everything; DPI must        │
     │ precede R2's visual verification)                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Windows PMv2 DPI awareness —                                         │
     │ SetProcessDpiAwarenessContext(PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2) at startup before   │
     │ any HWND (simplest correct mechanism; embedded manifest optional        │
     │ later); handle WM_DPICHANGED/WM_DISPLAYCHANGE by re-enumerating         │
     │ monitors + rebuilding overlay windows. Files:                           │
     │ crates/honk-platform-windows/src/lib.rs, src/runtime/windows.rs.        │
     │ 2. Non-blocking collect-window — replace the ≤3s spawn/poll + 60ms      │
     │ sleeps with a per-tick polled state machine (deadline-based); track the │
     │ Notepad child (process handle), WM_CLOSE→terminate fallback on          │
     │ close/stop; Drop for ControlledWindow::Notepad. Fixes sim freeze + IPC  │
     │ TIMEOUT + zombie leak. Files: honk-platform-windows/lib.rs              │
     │ (CollectWindowController), runtime/windows.rs.                          │
     │ 3. Unix singleton liveness — replace create_new lockfile with advisory  │
     │ flock (auto-released on process death; rustix unix-only dep in          │
     │ honk-control). Files: honk-control/src/platform.rs, its Cargo.toml.     │
     │ 4. macOS pump + present safety — drain NSApp events                     │
     │ (nextEventMatchingMask loop) instead of bare updateWindows(); make      │
     │ NSBitmapImageRep own its pixel buffer (copy-in) instead of wrapping the │
     │ reused Vec pointer. Files: honk-platform-macos/src/lib.rs.              │
     │ 5. Linux degraded-overlay honesty — HeadlessOverlay fallback only under │
     │ explicit HONK300_ALLOW_HEADLESS=1 (CI smoke); otherwise fail start      │
     │ loudly. Add an overlay capability to the status protocol so             │
     │ degraded/failed render states show in status + TUI Status tab. Files:   │
     │ honk-platform-linux/lib.rs, runtime/linux.rs, honk-control/protocol.rs, │
     │ TUI status. (+cheap X11 nits: cache the XFixes input region, re-set     │

     edit
     honk300 Full Evaluation + Renderer Rewrite + Cross-Platform Launch Plan

     ▎ STATUS: FINAL. Evaluation complete (4 Explore agents + firsthand reads + 
     ▎ 1 Plan agent). Verdict up front: the program is architecturally sound and
     ▎ honestly built — platform-free engine, real macOS backend, honest 
     ▎ capability degradation, accurate website. The gaps are: a crude 
     ▎ renderer/animator (the marquee fix), ~10 concrete reliability defects, 
     ▎ missing macOS packaging/lifecycle, and small config/TUI/site gaps. Plan =
     ▎ 4 execution rounds + a deferred bucket.

     Context

     honk300 is a from-scratch Rust reimplementation of Desktop Goose (M0–M19
     implemented by Codex 5.5, v0.1.0 released). Emmett is handing the driver's
     seat to Fable and wants:

     1. Full evaluation — reliability, consistency, feature parity across
     Windows / Linux / macOS.
     2. Renderer rework — the goose "looks like a few SVGs hobbled together, not
     animated very well." Make it look better, cross-platform.
     3. Program correctness — everything functions as intended:
     install/uninstall smooth, terminal commands (start/stop/reload/do/status)
     work, the TUI works, config options all present.
     4. Launch on all three platforms — Windows, Linux, macOS.

     Locked scoping decisions (from Emmett, this session)

     - Architectures: x64 + ARM64 only. No 32-bit (i686) targets. macOS can't do
     32-bit anyway.
     - macOS: full CI packaging now (universal/per-arch .app + DMG built +
     smoke-tested on GitHub macOS runners), hands-on verification structured as 
     a documented checklist to run when a Mac is available ("1 & 3" — CI-only
     for now, Mac coming later).
     - Also in scope: the website at C:\Users\hey\git\desktop-goose-site —
     evaluate + improve.

     Pre-existing board state (.tasks/TASKS.md)

     - #r2v (Backlog): Renderer V2 — Codex's M7.2 spike picked a "custom CPU
     sprite/atlas blitter". Subject to re-evaluation this plan.
     - #c0d (Backlog): Refine goose rendering + UI polish toward the original's
     feel.
     - #p4d (Backlog): fullscreen overlay present-cost measurement/optimization.
     - #a6e (Backlog): native Wayland full-support research.
     - #b9e (To-Do): default-OFF spicy behaviors (untracked thinking doc
     exists).
     - #m16r (Active): macOS Accessibility evidence — blocked on Mac hardware.
     - M19 packaging closed: Windows x64/ARM64 MSI+EXE (Global/Corporate),
     shell/PS installers, Linux archives. macOS packaging deferred (ADR 0013).

     Known repo facts

     - Workspace: honk-engine (platform-free, forbid(unsafe)), honk-control
     (IPC), honk-config, honk-config-tui (ratatui),
     honk-platform-{windows,macos,linux}, root honk300 bin.
     - Renderer today: procedural rig → tiny_skia::Pixmap (plan §7 render.rs # 
     Rig -> tiny_skia::Pixmap, dirty-rect aware), golden-frame tests.
     - 120 Hz sim; present on-dirty rate-capped; one overlay HWND per monitor
     (Windows).
     - Local gate: fmt --check, clippy -D warnings, test --workspace, build
     --release. Rust 1.95, edition 2021.
     - Changelog rule: CHANGELOG.md + HUMAN_CHANGELOG.md in lockstep, same
     commit.
     - Terminal windows protected (never manipulated). CLI/TUI-only control (no
     tray).

     Findings (to fill from exploration)

     A. Engine + renderer (agent 1 + firsthand read of render.rs/rig.rs)

     Tech stack is sound; the artistry/animation is the problem.
     - Renderer: crates/honk-engine/src/render.rs — tiny-skia CPU vector
     rasterization, anti-aliased, back-to-front painter: stipple shadow → neck
     capsule → body (closed cubic-bezier path in (fwd,across) local space) →
     head capsule → feet (flat 5-point polygons) → beak (bezier wedge) → eye
     (disc + highlight). Palette-driven (M15 RGB editing preserved).
     render_rig_with_palette at render.rs:342.
     - Rig: rig.rs::Rig::update(center, direction_deg, neck_lerp, gait_phase) —
     verified Exports.cs constants asserted in tests (rig.rs:162), but ~9 of
     them (UnderBody*, HeadRadius1, IPD, NeccHeight2…) are asserted yet unused 
     by the draw code; underbody_center computed but never drawn (dead
     geometry).
     - Animation weaknesses (why it "looks like hobbled SVGs"):
       a. Feet are stateless sine (feet.rs::gait) — the original C#
     ProceduralFeets is a stateful plant-and-swing with overshoot (foot stays
     planted, then swings ahead). Current feet slide/moonwalk. Biggest fidelity
     gap.
       b. Neck is one straight capsule neck_base→neck_head — no S-curve, no
     head-kept-level articulation.
       c. Whole-body rotation with direction + minimal secondary motion: body
     bob is abs(sin)*1.5 only; no head bob, no breathing idle, no blink, no tail
     geometry.
       d. neck_lerp applied linearly (no easing).
     - Golden tests: tests/golden.rs +
     tests/golden/{rest,reaching,mid_stride}.png are self-generated — any
     renderer rewrite re-blesses them (they pin determinism, not art quality).
     - Renderer V2 pre-decision: board #r2v + ADR 0001 chose a "CPU sprite/atlas
     blitter" — but that conflicts with plan §5.2 "no sprite art" guardrail
     (honk300_plan.md:281-285) and would sacrifice live palette editing +
     resolution independence. Recommendation: supersede with "Procedural Vector 
     V2" — keep tiny-skia, rewrite rig assembly + animation (planted feet,
     bezier neck, refined silhouette w/ tail, easing, blink/idle motion). New
     ADR.
     - Deck RNG: faithful-biased port is the locked decision (C8), pinned by
     test.
     - Extra agent-1 findings: mood-driven neck posture snaps un-damped
     (world.rs:835-849); ADR 0001:57-63 is where the sprite/atlas V2 direction
     lives (to supersede). Nuance: C# ProceduralFeets public API exposes only
     fields + 3 constants (Exports.cs:316-326) — the plant/swing update math is
     in the closed binary, so the V2 gait is a clean-room stateful
     reconstruction (plant → swing with 0.4 overshoot when lag > 5px) guided by
     those constants, not a literal port.

     ART DIRECTION (locked by Emmett, 2026-07-07 — reference images supplied):
     Reference SVGs/PNGs in C:\Users\hey\Downloads\gooooose_sprite_examples\ +
     C:\Users\hey\Downloads\a_flat_goose_sprite_019f02b5-6d09-7a9b-b3b4-1bcf3b5f
     bdc3.svg (Emmett's own generated art — safe to adapt path data directly;
     copy into docs/art-reference/ during implementation). Target =
     flat-illustration quality, "doesn't need to be exact, but that level of
     effort":
     1. Layered dark-slate wing with scalloped feather notches (new palette
     slot).
     2. Two-tone beak: orange + darker-orange underside/shading + nostril mark.
     3. Subtle body shading: throat/neck-front shade, underbody shade,
     behind-wing highlight (light-grey shade tone).
     4. Separate orange legs (shin + ankle bend) with webbed feet, two-tone.
     5. Tail tip with shade; long tapered S-neck widening into shoulders.
     6. Dual-view rig: side profile (h-mirrored) for shallow headings +
     idle/interactions; top-down view (supplied as reference #2) for steep
     headings, rotating freely to heading; quick crossfade (~100-150ms) at
     threshold (~±50° from horizontal). Replaces the current
     full-rotation-of-side-profile behavior that looks wrong walking up/down.
     7. Palette: 3 → ~6 configurable tones (goose_white, goose_shade,
     goose_wing, goose_orange, goose_orange_dark, goose_outline) — [colors]
     config + TUI RGB rows extended back-compatibly; new softer defaults
     matching the reference (off-white body, slate wing).
     8. Detail must read at desktop size (~60-90px tall) — consider 2x
     supersampled goose raster downscaled on present.

     B. Platform parity + reliability (agent 2 — full report received)

     Architecture: No shared Rust trait — capability contract is duck-typed
     same-named modules; the three runtime loops
     (src/runtime/{windows,macos,linux}.rs) are hand-cloned copies. Capability
     flows: BackendCapability{Supported,Unsupported,Denied,Failed} → collapsed
     to bools in WorldOptions; user-facing CapabilityStatus over IPC.

     Parity matrix (Impl/Deg/Perm/N-A/Miss):

     ┌────────────────────┬───────────┬───────────────┬─────────┬─────────────┐
     │      Feature       │    Win    │     macOS     │   X11   │   Wayland   │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │                    │ Impl (1   │ Impl (NSWindo │ Impl    │ Deg (single │
     │ Overlay render     │ win/monit │ w/display)    │ (ARGB   │  1280×720   │
     │                    │ or)       │               │ dock)   │ layer)      │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │                    │           │               │ Deg     │ N/A (click- │
     │ Per-pixel hit test │ Impl      │ Deg (bbox)    │ (XShape │ through)    │
     │                    │           │               │  bbox)  │             │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ Multi-monitor      │ Impl      │ Impl          │ Impl    │ N/A         │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ Cursor nab         │ Impl      │ Perm (AX)     │ Impl    │ N/A         │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ Window ride        │ Impl      │ Perm (AX)     │ Impl    │ N/A         │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ Collect windows    │ Impl      │ Impl/Perm     │ N/A     │ N/A         │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │                    │ Impl      │               │ ffplay/ │             │
     │ Sound              │ rodio     │ afplay shell  │ mpv     │ same        │
     │                    │           │               │ shell   │             │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ Presence           │ Impl      │ N/A           │ N/A     │ N/A         │
     │ (DND/fullscreen)   │           │               │         │             │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ DPI awareness      │ MISS      │ Impl (points) │ n/a     │ n/a         │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ IPC / TUI          │ Impl      │ Impl          │ Impl    │ Impl        │
     ├────────────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼─────────┼─────────────┤
     │ Install/Uninstall/ │           │ MISS (returns │         │             │
     │ Update             │ Impl      │  Err —        │ Impl    │ Impl        │
     │                    │           │ deferred)     │         │             │
     └────────────────────┴───────────┴───────────────┴─────────┴─────────────┘

     Ranked reliability concerns:
     1. Windows collect-window blocks the single render/sim thread up to ~3s
     (notepad spawn+poll, honk-platform-windows/lib.rs:458-466,649-668) — sim
     freezes AND IPC times out (2s RESPONSE_TIMEOUT). Windows-only freeze.
     2. No Windows DPI awareness — no manifest/PMv2/WM_DPICHANGED (plan mandates
     it, honk300_plan.md:772). Mixed-DPI → mispositioned/blurry overlay, cursor
     skew. Largest Windows correctness gap.
     3. Notepad process/window leak — ControlledWindow::Notepad has no
     Drop/DestroyWindow (lib.rs:543-545,589-595); collected Notepads survive
     stop.
     4. Unix stale singleton lock — create_new lockfile, no PID/flock liveness
     check (honk-control/platform.rs:335-368); crash ⇒ false "already running"
     on macOS/Linux. Windows named mutex unaffected.
     5. macOS Overlay::pump is bare updateWindows() — no NSApp event drain
     (lib.rs:215-218); collect-window close buttons unserviced.
     6. Linux silent headless zombie — overlay-creation failure silently falls
     back to no-op HeadlessOverlay (lib.rs:149-173); status says "running",
     nothing renders, eprintln discarded when detached.
     7. IPC single-client, blocking; slow sim ⇒ all control commands TIMEOUT.
     8. macOS bitmap over reused external Vec plane ptr (lib.rs:419-452) —
     AppKit doesn't copy; tearing/UAF risk.
     9. Linux X11 per-frame XFixes region alloc/destroy + event-mask re-set
     churn.
     10. Pervasive swallowed failures (let _ = + one-shot eprintln invisible
     when detached).

     Non-issues: hot loops avoid unwrap/expect; CommandServer joined on Drop.
     macOS code is REAL (objc2 AppKit, universal2 .app CI-proven for
     permissionless surface); Accessibility-granted mischief evidence pending a
     pre-granted Mac (#m16r). macOS presence hard-unsupported. Linux session
     detection: X11 whenever DISPLAY exists; --wayland opt-in; collect
     unsupported on all Linux.

     C. Packaging / config / control / TUI (agent 3 tail notes + firsthand 
     reads)

     Built targets today (cross-verified by agent 4 against real v0.1.0 release
     assets):
     - cargo-dist (release.yml, generated v0.31, driven by
     [workspace.metadata.dist].targets in root Cargo.toml):
     x86_64/aarch64-pc-windows-msvc ZIPs,
     x86_64/aarch64-unknown-linux-{gnu,musl} tar.xz, honk300-installer.sh +
     honk300-installer.ps1.
     - Hand-authored windows-installers.yml: Global/Corporate MSI (wix/,
     wix-corporate/) + EXE (inno/) for x64+ARM64, all with .sha256 sidecars. 14
     Windows+Linux assets total; zero macOS assets (script/package_macos_app.sh
     exists for local staging only).
     - Adding macOS = add 2 apple triples to dist targets (tar.xz +
     shell-installer pickup ~free) + hand-authored macos job building universal2
     .app + DMG (hdiutil) + sha256, unsigned personal-use.

     Lifecycle gaps:
     - macOS install/uninstall stubbed → Err (src/install.rs:134-137,194-197);
     update hard-errors on macOS (update.rs:150-156). Windows/Linux implemented
     (LOCALAPPDATA/XDG, PATH, Start Menu/desktop+autostart, 3 name aliases).
     - Non-purge uninstall deletes user memes/notes without backup
     (install.rs:158,183) — data-loss footgun.
     - update blocked on 32-bit hosts (fine — we're x64/ARM64 only).

     Config/TUI gaps: behavior.attack_randomly persisted but never mapped in
     effective_options (dead key — it's an original-parity feature [spontaneous
     cursor attacks], so WIRE it into the roam deck gated default-off rather
     than delete); mouse.grab_distance/drop_distance engine-wired but not
     TUI-editable.

     Config schema (firsthand, honk-config/src/lib.rs:24-370): 15 sections —
     behavior (silence_sounds, can_attack_mouse, attack_randomly,
     use_custom_colors, first/min/max_wandering_time_seconds), colors
     (goose_white/orange/outline hex), speeds (walk/run/charge_speed,
     acceleration_normal/charged, step_time_normal/charged, stop_radius), mud (3
     timings), mouse (grab/drop_distance, succ_time), behaviors
     (on_hour_double_honk, multi_monitor_chase), moods (dynamic_moods,
     mood_intensity), mischief (perch_and_ride, collect_windows/notes/memes),
     interaction (pat_streak), schedule (quiet_hours_enabled, quiet_start/end,
     dnd_respect, seasonal, autumn), appearance (calm_goose), audio (enabled,
     honk, bite, mud, pat), safety (pause_on_fullscreen, no_mouse_steal,
     no_window_ride), platform (wayland). Versioned (goose_config_version),
     unknown-key-tolerant.

     IPC: named pipe (Win) / Unix socket; single-client, 2s RESPONSE_TIMEOUT;
     Unix singleton = create_new lockfile, no liveness check (stale-lock false
     positive after crash — honk-control/platform.rs:335-368).

     Spicy behaviors doc (docs/thinking/2026-07-07-b9e-spicy-behaviors-plan.md,
     untracked): a complete, sound architecture plan for the default-OFF #b9e
     spicy family (separate engine spicy.rs contract, [spicy] config all-false,
     finite pokes, overlay-only visuals first, clipboard last, ADR 0014). Out of
     scope for this effort — keep queued; the doc should be committed (or at
     least preserved) as the #b9e handoff.

     Orchestration model (per Emmett, 2026-07-07)

     Fable (this session) is the orchestrator and conserves its own
     context/tokens:
     - Fable hands-on: the renderer, engine, and animation work — R2 in its
     entirety (rig, feet, views, part geometry, easing/secondary motion, palette
     wiring, golden strategy) plus the engine-touching piece of R1.6
     (attack_randomly deck wiring). This is the marquee creative/correctness
     work Emmett wants Fable's judgment on directly.
     - Subagents for everything else: R1 fixes (except the engine bit), R3 macOS
     packaging/lifecycle, R4 website — delegated to subagents per the standing
     model policy: Opus 4.8 [1m] at xhigh (default) or Sonnet 5 at max
     (mechanical fan-out). Never below that floor; never Haiku/other classes.
     - Fable reviews everything afterwards: each delegated round ends with a
     Fable review pass (diff review + verification evidence check) before
     Emmett's commit go-ahead. Delegation briefs must instruct subagents to
     return complete final-message reports (this session's explorers repeatedly
     truncated their hand-backs — require the full report pasted in the last
     message).
     - Context conservation: subagents do the file-heavy lifting; Fable reads
     diffs/evidence, not whole trees; board + task files carry cross-round state
     so context loss is survivable.

     The Plan — execution rounds

     Each round: branch off main, keep .tasks/TASKS.md + task files current,
     CHANGELOG.md + HUMAN_CHANGELOG.md in lockstep, local gate (fmt --check /
     clippy -D warnings / test --workspace / build --release), pause for
     Emmett's go-ahead before committing (his standing preference). Windows GUI
     changes get visual verification with screenshots before claiming done.

     supersede #r2v, fold #c0d into the Renderer V2 task.

     R1 — Reliability + correctness fixes (gates everything; DPI must precede 
     R2's visual verification)

     1. Windows PMv2 DPI awareness —
     SetProcessDpiAwarenessContext(PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2) at startup before any
     HWND (simplest correct mechanism; embedded manifest optional later); handle
     WM_DPICHANGED/WM_DISPLAYCHANGE by re-enumerating monitors + rebuilding
     overlay windows. Files: crates/honk-platform-windows/src/lib.rs,
     src/runtime/windows.rs.
     2. Non-blocking collect-window — replace the ≤3s spawn/poll + 60ms sleeps
     with a per-tick polled state machine (deadline-based); track the Notepad
     child (process handle), WM_CLOSE→terminate fallback on close/stop; Drop for
     ControlledWindow::Notepad. Fixes sim freeze + IPC TIMEOUT + zombie leak.
     Files: honk-platform-windows/lib.rs (CollectWindowController),
     runtime/windows.rs.
     3. Unix singleton liveness — replace create_new lockfile with advisory
     flock (auto-released on process death; rustix unix-only dep in
     honk-control). Files: honk-control/src/platform.rs, its Cargo.toml.
     4. macOS pump + present safety — drain NSApp events (nextEventMatchingMask
     loop) instead of bare updateWindows(); make NSBitmapImageRep own its pixel
     buffer (copy-in) instead of wrapping the reused Vec pointer. Files:
     honk-platform-macos/src/lib.rs.
     5. Linux degraded-overlay honesty — HeadlessOverlay fallback only under
     explicit HONK300_ALLOW_HEADLESS=1 (CI smoke); otherwise fail start loudly.
     Add an overlay capability to the status protocol so degraded/failed render
     states show in status + TUI Status tab. Files: honk-platform-linux/lib.rs,
     runtime/linux.rs, honk-control/protocol.rs, TUI status. (+cheap X11 nits:
     cache the XFixes input region, re-set only on bbox change; set event mask
     once.)
     6. Config/TUI/lifecycle gaps — wire behavior.attack_randomly into the roam
     deck (spontaneous nab, gated on can_attack_mouse + warp capability, default
     off — original-parity); TUI rows for mouse.grab_distance/drop_distance;
     non-purge uninstall must leave user memes/notes untouched (purge keeps
     existing backup semantics). Files: honk-engine (world/task), honk-config,
     honk-config-tui, src/install.rs.
     - ADR 0015 (reliability/platform-safety contract). Sync
     README/AGENTS/CLAUDE/honk300_plan where behavior changes.
     - Verify: local gate; Windows smoke — collect note/meme while status stays
     responsive (no TIMEOUT), stop leaves no Notepad zombie, DPI context
     assertion + visual position check; WSL/WSLg — kill -9 then restart (no
     stale-lock false positive), headless fallback errors loudly without the env
     var; cargo check all 8 apple/linux/windows triples; CI smoke scripts
     green.

     R2 — Renderer V2: "Procedural Vector V2" (the marquee; supersedes ADR

     goose per Emmett's reference SVGs — layered slate wing with feather

     threshold with hysteresis); palette 3→6 tones with reference-derived
     defaults (body #EDEDED, shade #C6C6C6, wing #515557, orange #FC7927, dark
     orange #D1551B, thin subtle outline kept for desktop contrast); adapt
     reference SVG path data into parametric part-local bezier definitions.
     - Engine (honk-engine, stays platform-free):
       - feet.rs: stateful FeetState — clean-room plant-and-swing (foot stays
     planted; swings with overshoot 0.4 when lag > 5px; step time from
     speeds.step_time_normal/charged); foot orientation along swing; no more
     sine slide.
       - rig.rs: Rig::update takes a RigInput/persistent RigState (heading, neck
     lerp, FeetState, view blend, blink/breath phases, smoothed mood posture).
     Dead geometry (underbody_center) either wired into the belly path or
     removed; the 9 asserted-unused constants reconciled.
       - render.rs: part-builder organization (side view + top-down view path
     sets, palette-driven, painter's order per view); optional 2x supersample of
     the goose raster (evaluate vs plain AA at 60–90px).
       - world.rs: damped (exp-smoothed) mood posture, blink timer, idle
     breathing, honk tail-flick.
     - Config/TUI: [colors] +3 keys (serde-defaulted, back-compat),

     - Tests: re-bless goldens (rest/reaching/mid-stride + new top-down +

● Write(.tasks\tasks\r2v.md)
  ⎿  Added 44 lines, removed 23 lines
       1 -TL;DR: Build Renderer V2 as a custom CPU sprite/atlas renderer 
         -that outputs premultiplied pixels for every platform backend, w
         -hile keeping `honk-engine` platform-free and the goose art clea
         -n-room.                                                        
       1 +TT;DR: Rewrite how the goose is drawn and animated — same cheap
         + vector tech, far better art and motion — so it looks like a pr
         +oper flat-illustration goose (per Emmett's reference art) inste
         +ad of a few hobbled shapes, on every platform.                 
       2  
       3 -## Why                                                         
       3 +## Direction change (2026-07-07)                               
       4  
       5 -M7 proved the current procedural renderer can be pushed closer 
         -to the original Desktop Goose, but it still costs too much iter
         -ation effort for visual polish. The accepted direction is to st
         -op endlessly tuning per-frame geometric paths and instead give 
         -the project an explicit sprite/atlas rendering contract that ca
         -n preserve anchors, hit masks, animation frames, and future cus
         -tomization.                                                    
       5 +This task originally recorded the M7.2 sprite/atlas blitter dec
         +ision. **Superseded by Emmett's                                
       6 +approval of "Procedural Vector V2"** (Fable evaluation session)
         +: keep tiny-skia per-frame vector                              
       7 +rendering (bbox ~120px is trivially cheap; premultiplied RGBA a
         +lready fits every backend present                              
       8 +path), rewrite the rig assembly + animation instead. Rationale:
         + a sprite atlas conflicted with the                            
       9 +plan §5.2 no-sprite-art guardrail, would complicate M15 live RG
         +B palette editing (re-bake on                                  
      10 +change), and rotation would need many baked angles. ADR 0014 re
         +cords this and supersedes                                      
      11 +ADR 0001's Renderer Direction. Old `#c0d` (rendering polish) is
         + folded in here.                                               
      12  
       7 -## Context                                                     
      13 +## Art direction (locked, reference images from Emmett)        
      14  
       9 -- Spawned from M7.2 after comparing renderer options against th
         -e deployment targets.                                          
      10 -- Windows remains first, and the current presentation path stil
         -l needs premultiplied BGRA pixels for `UpdateLayeredWindow`.   
      11 -- macOS, Linux X11, Linux Wayland-degraded mode, x64, and ARM/A
         -RM64 should consume the same renderer output without forcing OS
         - handles or GPU surfaces into `honk-engine`.                   
      12 -- `tiny-skia` and `resvg` remain useful for vector effects or a
         -sset rasterization, but the main goose runtime should not depen
         -d on per-frame path reconstruction.                            
      13 -- Avoid Vello/wgpu, Skia, Bevy, Macroquad, and ggez as the core
         - runtime renderer for the desktop-pet overlay. They are useful 
         -tools in other contexts, but they add GPU/windowing/build compl
         -exity that does not fit the layered-overlay pixel path cleanly.
      15 +References in `docs/art-reference/` (Emmett's own Quiver AI SVG
         +s — path data may be adapted                                   
      16 +directly; original-app assets remain off-limits). Target: flat-
         +illustration quality —                                         
      17 +1. Layered dark-slate wing with scalloped feather notches.     
      18 +2. Two-tone beak (orange + darker underside/nostril).          
      19 +3. Body shading: throat, underbody, behind-wing (light-grey sha

       75 +impl Foot {                                                   
       76 +    fn planted(pos: Vec2, heading: Vec2) -> Self {
