       83 +                                                              
       84 +    fn lift(&self) -> f32 {                                   
       85 +        match &self.swing {                                   
       86 +            Some(s) => (s.t * std::f32::consts::PI).sin() * MA
          +X_LIFT,                                                       
       87 +            None => 0.0,                                      
       88 +        }                                                     
       89 +    }                                                         
       90 +                                                              
       91 +    fn pose(&self) -> FootPose {                              
       92 +        FootPose {                                            
       93 +            pos: self.pos,                                    
       94 +            lift: self.lift(),                                
       95 +            heading: self.heading,                            
       96 +            swinging: self.swing.is_some(),                   
       97 +        }                                                     
       98 +    }                                                         
       99 +}                                                             
      100 +                                                              
      101 +/// Persistent plant-and-swing state for both feet.           
      102 +#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]                            
      103 +pub struct FeetState {                                        
      104 +    left: Foot,                                               
      105 +    right: Foot,                                              
      106 +    /// Feet planted since the last drain (world stamps footma
          +rks here).                                                    
      107 +    plants: Vec<Vec2>,                                        
      108 +}                                                             
      109 +                                                              
      110 +impl FeetState {                                              
      111 +    /// Both feet planted at their home positions under `cente
          +r`, facing `forward`.                                         
      112 +    pub fn new(center: Vec2, forward: Vec2) -> Self {         
      113 +        let (home_l, home_r) = homes(center, forward);        
      114 +        Self {                                                
      115 +            left: Foot::planted(home_l, forward),             
      116 +            right: Foot::planted(home_r, forward),            
      117 +            plants: Vec::new(),                               
      118 +        }                                                     
      119 +    }                                                         
      120 +                                                              
      121 +    /// Advance the gait one tick. `center` is the entity grou
          +nd point, `forward` the                                       
      122 +    /// facing unit vector, `velocity` the current velocity (p
          +x/s), and `step_time` the                                     
      123 +    /// entity's per-step interval (`<= 0` falls back to the w
          +alk default).                                                 
      124 +    pub fn tick(&mut self, dt: f32, center: Vec2, forward: Vec
          +2, velocity: Vec2, step_time: f32) {                          
      125 +        let duration = if step_time > 1e-3 {                  
      126 +            step_time                                         
      127 +        } else {                                              
      128 +            DEFAULT_STEP_TIME                                 
      129 +        };                                                    
      130 +        let (home_l, home_r) = homes(center, forward);        
      131 +                                                              
      132 +        // Teleport guard: far past any plausible stride, both
          + feet snap home.                                              
      133 +        if Vec2::distance(self.left.pos, home_l) > SNAP_DISTAN
          +CE                                                            
      134 +            || Vec2::distance(self.right.pos, home_r) > SNAP_D
          +ISTANCE                                                       
      135 +        {                                                     
      136 +            self.left = Foot::planted(home_l, forward);       
      137 +            self.right = Foot::planted(home_r, forward);      
      138 +            return;                                           
      139 +        }                                                     
      140 +                                                              
      141 +        // Advance any active swing.                          
      142 +        for (foot, home) in [(&mut self.left, home_l), (&mut s
          +elf.right, home_r)] {                                         
      143 +            let _ = home;                                     
      144 +            if let Some(swing) = &mut foot.swing {            
      145 +                swing.t = (swing.t + dt / swing.duration).min(
          +1.0);                                                         
      146 +                let eased = smoothstep(swing.t);              
      147 +                foot.pos = Vec2::lerp(swing.from, swing.to, ea
          +sed);                                                         
      148 +                let dir = swing.to - swing.from;              
      149 +                if dir.magnitude() > 1e-3 {                   
      150 +                    foot.heading = dir.normalized();          
      151 +                }                                             
      152 +                if swing.t >= 1.0 {                           
      153 +                    foot.swing = None;                        
      154 +                    self.plants.push(foot.pos);               
      155 +                }                                             
      156 +            }                                                 
      157 +        }                                                     
      158 +                                                              
      159 +        // Trigger the next step: one foot in the air at a tim
          +e, farthest-lagging first.                                    
      160 +        let airborne = self.left.swing.is_some() || self.right
          +.swing.is_some();                                             
      161 +        if !airborne {                                        
      162 +            let lag_l = Vec2::distance(self.left.pos, home_l);
      163 +            let lag_r = Vec2::distance(self.right.pos, home_r)
          +;                                                             
      164 +            let (foot, home, lag) = if lag_l >= lag_r {       
      165 +                (&mut self.left, home_l, lag_l)               
      166 +            } else {                                          
      167 +                (&mut self.right, home_r, lag_r)              
      168 +            };                                                
      169 +            if lag > WANT_STEP_AT_DISTANCE {                  
      170 +                // Aim past home (overshoot) and lead a moving
          + body so the foot lands                                       
      171 +                // where the home will roughly be, not where i
          +t was.                                                        
      172 +                let dir = (home - foot.pos).normalized();     
      173 +                let target =                                  
      174 +                    home + dir * (lag * OVERSHOOT_FRACTION) + 
          +velocity * (duration * 0.5);                                  
      175 +                foot.swing = Some(Swing {                     
      176 +                    from: foot.pos,                           
      177 +                    to: target,                               
      178 +                    t: 0.0,                                   
      179 +                    duration,                                 
      180 +                });                                           
      181 +            }                                                 
      182 +        }                                                     
      183 +    }                                                         
      184 +                                                              
      185 +    /// Ground positions of both feet.                        
      186 +    pub fn positions(&self) -> Feet {                         
      187 +        Feet {                                                
      188 +            left: self.left.pos,                              
      189 +            right: self.right.pos,                            
      190 +        }                                                     
      191 +    }                                                         
      192 +                                                              
      193 +    /// Full render poses, `[left, right]`.                   
      194 +    pub fn poses(&self) -> [FootPose; 2] {                    
      195 +        [self.left.pose(), self.right.pose()]                 
      196 +    }                                                         
      197 +                                                              
      198 +    /// 0..1 progress of the currently swinging foot (0 when b
          +oth planted). Drives the                                      
      199 +    /// body bob so it rises mid-step.                        
      200 +    pub fn swing_progress(&self) -> f32 {                     
      201 +        [&self.left, &self.right]                             
      202 +            .iter()                                           
      203 +            .filter_map(|f| f.swing.as_ref().map(|s| s.t))    
      204 +            .fold(0.0, f32::max)                              
      205 +    }                                                         
      206 +                                                              
      207 +    /// Drain foot-plant events accumulated since the last cal
          +l (world → footmarks).                                        
      208 +    pub fn drain_plants(&mut self, mut f: impl FnMut(Vec2)) { 
      209 +        for p in self.plants.drain(..) {

      211 +        }                                                     
      212 +    }

      227 +fn smoothstep(t: f32) -> f32 {                                
      228 +    let t = t.clamp(0.0, 1.0);                                
      229 +    t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)

      239 +    fn blink_amount(&self, now: f32) -> f32 {                 
      240 +        let t = now - self.blink_started;                     
      241 +        if t < 0.0 {                                          
      242 +            0.0                                               
      243 +        } else if t < BLINK_CLOSE {                           
      244 +            t / BLINK_CLOSE                                   
      245 +        } else if t < BLINK_CLOSE + BLINK_OPEN {              
      246 +            1.0 - (t - BLINK_CLOSE) / BLINK_OPEN              
      247 +        } else {                                              
      248 +            0.0                                               
      249 +        }                                                     
      250 +    }

      252 +    /// Advance every animation channel one tick and assemble 
          +the drawable pose.                                            
      253 +    pub fn update(&mut self, input: &RigInput) -> GoosePose { 
      254 +        let forward = Vec2::from_angle_degrees(input.direction
          +_deg);                                                        
      255 +                                                              
      256 +        // Feet first: the gait is the ground truth the body r
          +eacts to.                                                     
      257 +        self.feet.tick(                                       
      258 +            input.dt,                                         
      259 +            input.center,                                     
      260              forward,
      120 -            neck_lerp_percent: p,                             
      121 -            body_center,                                      
      122 -            underbody_center,                                 
      123 -            neck_base,                                        
      124 -            neck_head,                                        
      125 -            snout_center,                                     
      126 -            beak_tip,                                         
      127 -            eye,                                              
      128 -            feet,                                             
      261 +            input.velocity,                                   
      262 +            input.step_time,                                  
      263 +        );                                                    
      264 +                                                              
      265 +        // Eased neck (no more mood snapping).                
      266 +        let k = 1.0 - (-NECK_SMOOTH_RATE * input.dt).exp();   
      267 +        self.neck += (input.neck_target.clamp(0.0, 1.0) - self
          +.neck) * k;                                                   
      268 +                                                              
      269 +        // Side-facing mirror: update only when the heading is
          + meaningfully horizontal.                                     
      270 +        if input.speed > 1.0 {                                
      271 +            if let Some(m) = side_mirror(forward) {           
      272 +                self.mirror = m;                              
      273 +            }                                                 
      274          }
      275 +                                                              
      276 +        // View selection with hysteresis; only a moving goose
          + can change views.                                            
      277 +        if input.speed > 1.0 {                                
      278 +            let steep_deg = forward.y.abs().asin().to_degrees(
          +);                                                            
      279 +            if self.top_active && steep_deg < TOPDOWN_EXIT_DEG
          + {                                                            
      280 +                self.top_active = false;                      
      281 +                self.fade = 1.0 - self.fade.min(1.0);         
      282 +            } else if !self.top_active && steep_deg > TOPDOWN_
          +ENTER_DEG {                                                   
      283 +                self.top_active = true;                       
      284 +                self.fade = 1.0 - self.fade.min(1.0);         
      285 +            }                                                 
      286 +        }                                                     
      287 +        if input.dt > 0.0 {                                   
      288 +            self.fade = (self.fade + input.dt / VIEW_FADE_SECS
          +).min(1.0);                                                   
      289 +        }                                                     
      290 +                                                              
      291 +        // Breathing fades out with speed; tail flick decays e
          +xponentially.                                                 
      292 +        let idle = (1.0 - input.speed / 40.0).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
      293 +        self.breath_phase += input.dt * std::f32::consts::TAU 
          +* BREATH_HZ;                                                  
      294 +        let breath = self.breath_phase.sin() * idle;          
      295 +        if input.dt > 0.0 {                                   
      296 +            self.tail_flick *= (-input.dt / TAIL_FLICK_DECAY).
          +exp();                                                        
      297 +            if self.tail_flick < 0.01 {                       
      298 +                self.tail_flick = 0.0;                        
      299 +            }                                                 
      300 +        }                                                     
      301 +                                                              
      302 +        let blink = self.blink_amount(input.now);             
      303 +        let shared = SharedChannels {                         
      304 +            forward,                                          
      305 +            neck: self.neck,                                  
      306 +            breath,                                           
      307 +            blink,                                            
      308 +            tail_flick: self.tail_flick,                      
      309 +        };                                                    
      310 +                                                              
      311 +        let primary_view = self.top_active;                   
      312 +        let primary = assemble(input, &self.feet, &shared, pri
          +mary_view, self.mirror);                                      
      313 +        let fading = if self.fade < 1.0 {                     
      314 +            let rig = assemble(input, &self.feet, &shared, !pr
          +imary_view, self.mirror);                                     
      315 +            Some((rig, 1.0 - self.fade))                      
      316 +        } else {                                              
      317 +            None                                              
      318 +        };                                                    
      319 +        GoosePose { primary, fading }                         
      320      }

      333 +/// Side-view mirror for a heading, if the heading is horizont
          +al enough to say                                              
      334 +/// (`None` inside the dead zone so the last facing sticks).  
      335 +fn side_mirror(forward: Vec2) -> Option<f32> {                
      336 +    if forward.x > 0.2 {                                      
      337 +        Some(1.0)                                             
      338 +    } else if forward.x < -0.2 {                              
      339 +        Some(-1.0)                                            
      340 +    } else {                                                  
      341 +        None                                                  
      342 +    }                                                         
      343 +}

      345 +/// Animation channels shared by both view assemblies in one f
          +rame.                                                         
      346 +struct SharedChannels {                                       
      347 +    forward: Vec2,                                            
      348 +    neck: f32,                                                
      349 +    breath: f32,                                              
      350 +    blink: f32,                                               
      351 +    tail_flick: f32,

      353 +                                                              
      354 +fn assemble(                                                  
      355 +    input: &RigInput,                                         
      356 +    feet: &FeetState,                                         
      357 +    ch: &SharedChannels,                                      
      358 +    top: bool,                                                
      359 +    mirror: f32,                                              
      360 +) -> Rig {                                                    
      361 +    if top {                                                  
      362 +        assemble_top(input, feet, ch)                         
      363 +    } else {                                                  
      364 +        assemble_side(input, feet, ch, mirror)                
      365 +    }                                                         
      366 +}                                                             
      367 +                                                              
      368 +/// Map a side-reference-art point (`goose-side-main.svg` coor
          +dinates, art faces                                            
      369 +/// screen-left) onto the screen for a goose standing at `grou
          +nd` with facing `mirror`.                                     
      370 +pub(crate) fn side_ref_pt(ground: Vec2, mirror: f32, rx: f32, 
          +ry: f32) -> Vec2 {                                            
      371 +    let fwd = (REF_GROUND.x - rx) * SIDE_SCALE; // + = toward 
          +the beak                                                      
      372 +    let up = (REF_GROUND.y - ry) * SIDE_SCALE; // + = above th
          +e ground line                                                 
      373 +    Vec2::new(ground.x + fwd * mirror * -1.0, ground.y - up)  
      374 +}                                                             
      375 +                                                              
      376 +fn assemble_side(input: &RigInput, feet: &FeetState, ch: &Shar
          +edChannels, mirror: f32) -> Rig {                             
      377 +    let ground = input.center;                                
      378 +    // Screen-forward for the side view: strictly horizontal, 
          +per the mirror.                                               
      379 +    let fwd = Vec2::new(mirror, 0.0);                         
      380 +    // `side_ref_pt` treats mirror=-1 as art-native (facing le
          +ft).                                                          
      381 +    let rp = |rx: f32, ry: f32| side_ref_pt(ground, mirror, rx
          +, ry);                                                        
      382 +                                                              
      383 +    // Body bob: rises mid-swing, plus a whisper of idle breat
          +hing.                                                         
      384 +    let bob = feet.swing_progress().sin_bob() + ch.breath * 0.
          +5;                                                            
      385 +    let bob_v = UP * bob;                                     
      386 +                                                              
      387 +    let body_center = rp(80.0, 112.0) + bob_v;                
      388 +    let underbody_center = rp(58.0, 132.0) + bob_v;           
      389 +                                                              
      390 +    // Neck spine: shoulder → head anchor, eased between tucke
          +d and raised.                                                 
      391 +    let p = ch.neck;                                          
      392 +    let eased = p * p * (3.0 - 2.0 * p);                      
      393 +    let neck_base = rp(50.0, 80.0) + bob_v;                   
      394 +    let head_tucked = rp(52.0, 52.0);                         
      395 +    let head_raised = rp(44.0, 20.0);                         
      396 +    // Arc the travel slightly forward so the raise reads orga
          +nic, not linear.                                              
      397 +    let arc = fwd * (10.0 * (eased * (1.0 - eased)) * 4.0 * SI
          +DE_SCALE);                                                    
      398 +    let neck_head = Vec2::lerp(head_tucked, head_raised, eased
          +) + arc + bob_v * 0.6;                                        
      399 +                                                              
      400 +    let len = Vec2::distance(neck_base, neck_head);           
      401 +    // S-curve: lower control bows backward, upper control bow
          +s forward.                                                    
      402 +    let neck_c1 = neck_base + UP * (len * 0.42) - fwd * (len *
          + 0.10);                                                       
      403 +    let neck_c2 = neck_head - UP * (len * 0.34) + fwd * (len *
          + 0.14);                                                       
      404 +                                                              
      405 +    // Head tilt: level when raised, dipping slightly when tuc
          +ked.                                                          
      406 +    let head_tilt = (1.0 - eased) * 0.16;                     
      407 +                                                              
      408 +    // Beak/eye offsets from the head anchor (44,20) in ref un
          +its, tilt-rotated.                                            
      409 +    let head_off = |rx: f32, ry: f32| {                       
      410 +        let dx = (44.0 - rx) * SIDE_SCALE; // + toward beak ti
          +p                                                             
      411 +        let dy = (20.0 - ry) * SIDE_SCALE; // + above head anc
          +hor                                                           
      412 +        let (s, c) = head_tilt.sin_cos();                     
      413 +        let fx = dx * c - (-dy) * s * 0.0 - 0.0; // rotation o
          +f (dx, up dy) about anchor                                    
      414 +        // Rotate (forward=dx, up=dy) by -tilt around the anch
          +or: forward stays forward,                                    
      415 +        // up tips toward forward as the head dips.           
      416 +        let f2 = dx * c + dy * s;                             
      417 +        let u2 = dy * c - dx * s;                             
      418 +        let _ = fx;                                           
      419 +        neck_head + fwd * f2 + UP * u2                        
      420 +    };                                                        
      421 +    let beak_tip = head_off(13.0, 26.0);                      
      422 +    let snout_center = head_off(28.0, 22.0);                  
      423 +    let eye = head_off(41.2, 17.0);                           
      424 +                                                              
      425 +    Rig {                                                     
      426 +        ground,                                               
      427 +        forward: ch.forward,                                  
      428 +        neck_lerp_percent: p,                                 
      429 +        view: RigView::Side { mirror },                       
      430 +        body_center,                                          
      431 +        underbody_center,                                     
      432 +        neck_base,                                            
      433 +        neck_c1,                                              
      434 +        neck_c2,                                              
      435 +        neck_head,                                            
      436 +        snout_center,                                         
      437 +        beak_tip,                                             
      438 +        eye,                                                  
      439 +        head_tilt,                                            
      440 +        feet: feet.positions(),                               
      441 +        feet_pose: feet.poses(),                              
      442 +        bob,                                                  
      443 +        breath: ch.breath,                                    
      444 +        blink: ch.blink,                                      
      445 +        tail_flick: ch.tail_flick,                            
      446 +    }                                                         
      447 +}                                                             
      448 +                                                              
      449 +fn assemble_top(input: &RigInput, feet: &FeetState, ch: &Share
          +dChannels) -> Rig {                                           
      450 +    let ground = input.center;                                
      451 +    let heading = if ch.forward.magnitude() > 1e-3 {          
      452 +        ch.forward                                            
      453 +    } else {                                                  
      454 +        Vec2::new(0.0, -1.0)                                  
      455 +    };                                                        
      456 +    // Waddle roll synced to the gait.                        
      457 +    let waddle = (feet.swing_progress() * std::f32::consts::PI
          +).sin() * 0.05;                                               
      458 +                                                              
      459 +    // Neck reach translates the head/beak forward along the h
          +eading.                                                       
      460 +    let p = ch.neck;                                          
      461 +    let eased = p * p * (3.0 - 2.0 * p);                      
      462 +    let reach = heading * (eased * 8.0);                      
      463 +                                                              
      464 +    let body_center = ground;                                 
      465 +    let neck_base = ground + heading * (43.0 * TOP_SCALE);    
      466 +    let neck_head = ground + heading * (57.5 * TOP_SCALE) + re
          +ach;                                                          
      467 +    let beak_tip = ground + heading * (85.7 * TOP_SCALE) + rea
          +ch;                                                           
      468 +    let snout_center = ground + heading * (76.0 * TOP_SCALE) +
          + reach;                                                       
      469 +    let mid = Vec2::lerp(neck_base, neck_head, 0.5);          
      470 +                                                              
      471 +    Rig {                                                     
      472 +        ground,                                               
      473 +        forward: ch.forward,                                  
      474 +        neck_lerp_percent: p,                                 
      475 +        view: RigView::TopDown { heading, waddle },           
      476 +        body_center,                                          
      477 +        underbody_center: body_center,                        
      478 +        neck_base,                                            
      479 +        neck_c1: mid,                                         
      480 +        neck_c2: mid,                                         
      481 +        neck_head,                                            
      482 +        snout_center,                                         
      483 +        beak_tip,                                             
      484 +        // Eyes sit on the head from above; expose the leading
          + edge point.                                                  
      485 +        eye: neck_head + heading * (6.0 * TOP_SCALE),         
      486 +        head_tilt: 0.0,                                       
      487 +        feet: feet.positions(),                               
      488 +        feet_pose: feet.poses(),                              
      489 +        bob: 0.0,                                             
      490 +        breath: ch.breath,                                    
      491 +        blink: ch.blink,                                      
      492 +        tail_flick: ch.tail_flick,                            
      493 +    }                                                         
      494 +}                                                             
      495 +                                                              
      496 +/// Small helper: turn a swing progress `0..1` into the body-b
          +ob height.                                                    
      497 +trait SinBob {                                                
      498 +    fn sin_bob(self) -> f32;                                  
      499 +}                                                             
      500 +impl SinBob for f32 {                                         
      501 +    fn sin_bob(self) -> f32 {                                 
      502 +        (self * std::f32::consts::PI).sin() * 1.2             
      503 +    }                                                         
      504 +}

      623 +        assert!(raised.neck_lerp_percent > 0.9);              
      624      }
      625  
      626      #[test]
      627 +    fn neck_eases_instead_of_snapping() {                     
      628 +        let center = Vec2::new(300.0, 300.0);                 
      629 +        let mut anim = RigAnim::new(center, 0.0);             
      630 +        let mut input = RigInput::static_pose(center, 0.0, 1.0
          +);                                                            
      631 +        input.dt = DT;                                        
      632 +        input.now = DT;                                       
      633 +        let after_one_tick = anim.update(&input).primary;     
      634 +        // One tick toward a raised neck moves only a fraction
          + of the way.                                                  
      635 +        assert!(after_one_tick.neck_lerp_percent < 0.2);      
      636 +    }                                                         
      637 +                                                              
      638 +    #[test]                                                   
      639 +    fn steep_heading_switches_to_top_down_with_fade() {       
      640 +        let center = Vec2::new(300.0, 300.0);                 
      641 +        let mut anim = RigAnim::new(center, 0.0);             
      642 +        // Establish the side view.                           
      643 +        let mut input = RigInput::static_pose(center, 0.0, 0.0
          +);                                                            
      644 +        input.dt = DT;                                        
      645 +        anim.update(&input);                                  
      646 +        assert!(!anim.top_view_active());                     
      647 +        // Dive straight down at speed: top-down should engage
          + with a crossfade.                                            
      648 +        let mut input = RigInput {                            
      649 +            center,                                           
      650 +            direction_deg: 90.0,                              
      651 +            neck_target: 0.0,                                 
      652 +            speed: 80.0,                                      
      653 +            velocity: Vec2::new(0.0, 80.0),                   
      654 +            step_time: 0.2,                                   
      655 +            now: DT,                                          
      656 +            dt: DT,                                           
      657 +        };                                                    
      658 +        let pose = anim.update(&input);                       
      659 +        assert!(anim.top_view_active());                      
      660 +        assert!(matches!(pose.primary.view, RigView::TopDown {
          + .. }));                                                      
      661 +        let (fading, alpha) = pose.fading.expect("crossfade in
          + progress");                                                  
      662 +        assert!(matches!(fading.view, RigView::Side { .. })); 
      663 +        assert!(alpha > 0.0 && alpha < 1.0);                  
      664 +        // The fade completes within VIEW_FADE_SECS.          
      665 +        for i in 2..30 {                                      
      666 +            input.now = i as f32 * DT;                        
      667 +            input.center = input.center + input.velocity * DT;
      668 +            let pose = anim.update(&input);                   
      669 +            if pose.fading.is_none() {                        
      670 +                assert!(input.now <= VIEW_FADE_SECS + 3.0 * DT
          +);                                                            
      671 +                return;                                       
      672 +            }                                                 
      673 +        }                                                     
      674 +        panic!("crossfade never completed");                  
      675 +    }                                                         
      676 +                                                              
      677 +    #[test]                                                   
      678 +    fn view_has_hysteresis() {                                
      679 +        let center = Vec2::new(300.0, 300.0);                 
      680 +        let mut anim = RigAnim::new(center, 0.0);             
      681 +        let mut tick = |deg: f32, anim: &mut RigAnim| {       
      682 +            let v = Vec2::from_angle_degrees(deg) * 80.0;     
      683 +            anim.update(&RigInput {                           
      684 +                center,                                       
      685 +                direction_deg: deg,                           
      686 +                neck_target: 0.0,                             
      687 +                speed: 80.0,                                  
      688 +                velocity: v,                                  
      689 +                step_time: 0.2,                               
      690 +                now: 0.0,                                     
      691 +                dt: DT,                                       
      692 +            });                                               
      693 +        };                                                    
      694 +        // 50° is inside the hysteresis band: entering from th
          +e side view, no switch.                                       
      695 +        tick(50.0, &mut anim);                                
      696 +        assert!(!anim.top_view_active());                     
      697 +        // 60° switches in; falling back to 50° must NOT switc
          +h out (exit is 45°).                                          
      698 +        tick(60.0, &mut anim);                                
      699 +        assert!(anim.top_view_active());                      
      700 +        tick(50.0, &mut anim);                                
      701 +        assert!(anim.top_view_active());                      
      702 +        tick(30.0, &mut anim);                                
      703 +        assert!(!anim.top_view_active());                     
      704 +    }                                                         
      705 +                                                              
      706 +    #[test]

      709 +        let mut anim = RigAnim::new(center, 0.0);

      715 +            speed: 80.0,

      717 +            step_time: 0.2,                                   
      718 +            now: DT,                                          
      719 +            dt: DT,                                           
      720 +        });                                                   
      721 +        let (fading, _) = pose.fading.expect("fade active");  
      722 +        let union = pose.bounding_box();                      
      723 +        let a = pose.primary.bounding_box();                  
      724 +        let b = fading.bounding_box();                        
      725 +        assert!(union.min.x <= a.min.x.min(b.min.x));         
      726 +        assert!(union.max.y >= a.max.y.max(b.max.y));         
      727 +    }

      207 +    use honk_engine::tiny_skia::{FilterQuality, PixmapPaint, T
          +ransform};                                                    
      208 +    const SMALL: u32 = 150;                                   
      209 +    const ZOOM: u32 = 2;                                      
      210 +    const BIG: u32 = SMALL * ZOOM;                            
      211 +    const FRAMES: u32 = 8;                                    
      212 +    let mut sheet = Pixmap::new(BIG * FRAMES, BIG).expect("she
          +et alloc");                                                   
      213 +    sheet.fill(Color::from_rgba8(0x2e, 0x33, 0x38, 0xff));    
      214 +    let pal = RenderPalette::default();                       
      215 +                                                              
      216 +    let mut s = Sim::new(0.0);                                
      217 +    s.run(0.4, 0.0, 80.0, 0.55, 0.2); // settle into the cycle
      218 +    for i in 0..FRAMES {                                      
      219 +        let pose = s.run(0.05, 0.0, 80.0, 0.55, 0.2);         
      220 +        let mut cell = Pixmap::new(SMALL, SMALL).expect("cell 
          +alloc");                                                      
      221 +        cell.fill(Color::TRANSPARENT);                        
      222 +        let anchor = Vec2::new(SMALL as f32 * 0.5, SMALL as f3
          +2 * 0.7);                                                     
      223 +        let origin = pose.primary.ground - anchor;            
      224 +        // World-fixed ground ticks every 10 px so foot slippa
          +ge is visible.                                                
      225 +        {                                                     
      226 +            use honk_engine::tiny_skia::Paint;                
      227 +            let mut tick = Paint::default();                  
      228 +            tick.set_color_rgba8(0xff, 0xd7, 0x00, 200);      
      229 +            let gy = pose.primary.ground.y;                   
      230 +            let mut wx = (origin.x / 10.0).floor() * 10.0;    
      231 +            while wx < origin.x + SMALL as f32 {              
      232 +                let px = wx - origin.x;                       
      233 +                let rect = honk_engine::tiny_skia::Rect::from_
          +xywh(px, gy - origin.y + 4.0, 1.5, 6.0);                      
      234 +                if let Some(r) = rect {                       
      235 +                    cell.fill_rect(r, &tick, Transform::identi
          +ty(), None);                                                  
      236 +                }                                             
      237 +                wx += 10.0;                                   
      238 +            }                                                 
      239 +        }                                                     
      240 +        render_pose_with_palette(&mut cell, &pose, origin, pal
          +);                                                            
      241 +        sheet.draw_pixmap(                                    
      242 +            0,                                                
      243 +            0,                                                
      244 +            cell.as_ref(),                                    
      245 +            &PixmapPaint {                                    
      246 +                quality: FilterQuality::Nearest,              
      247 +                ..PixmapPaint::default()                      
      248 +            },                                                
      249 +            Transform::from_scale(ZOOM as f32, ZOOM as f32).po
          +st_translate((i * BIG) as f32, 0.0),                          
      250 +            None,                                             
      251 +        );                                                    
      252 +    }                                                         
      253 +    sheet.save_png(out).expect("write walk strip");           
      254 +    println!("wrote {out} ({}x{})", sheet.width(), sheet.heigh
          +t());                                                         
      255 +}

       78 +fn walk(anim: &mut RigAnim, start: Vec2, dir_deg: f32, speed: 
          +f32, neck: f32, secs: f32) -> Rig {                           
       79 +    let velocity = Vec2::from_angle_degrees(dir_deg) * speed; 
       80 +    let ticks = (secs / DT).round().max(1.0) as usize;        
       81 +    let mut center = start;                                   
       82 +    let mut now = 0.0;                                        
       83 +    let mut rig = None;                                       
       84 +    for _ in 0..ticks {                                       
       85 +        now += DT;                                            
       86 +        center = center + velocity * DT;                      
       87 +        rig = Some(                                           
       88 +            anim.update(&RigInput {                           
       89 +                center,

       91 +                neck_target: neck,

       99 +        );                                                    
      100 +    }                                                         
      101 +    rig.expect("at least one tick")                           
      102 +}

> Make sure you take a thorough look at the actions as well, like grabbing      
  windows and tracking mud, which should be random. You shouldn't track mud     
  constantly. That should only happen at random times.                          
                                                                                
  Even while the program is active, after 5 minutes or so, the goose should     
  waddle off-screen, up or down, preferably left to right. That way, if the     
  user has multiple monitors, the goose walks off-screen, disappears, and then  
  comes back 1.5 to 2 minutes later, kind of randomized. This gives the sense   
  that the goose is going to do something else. Sometimes, when he comes back,  
  he'll pull extra windows in or pull memes or things.

      200  
      201 +/// Why the goose is leaving the screen, and what it brings ba
          +ck (ADR 0016).                                                
      202 +#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]                      
      203 +pub enum ExcursionKind {                                      
      204 +    /// A long "errand" — gone ~1.5–2 minutes; the world may c
          +hain a collect-window                                         
      205 +    /// prank onto the return.                                
      206 +    Errand,                                                   
      207 +    /// A quick hop just past the edge; the goose comes back s
          +econds later tracking                                         
      208 +    /// mud for `mud_secs` — as if it found a puddle out there
          +.                                                             
      209 +    Puddle { mud_secs: f32 },                                 
      210 +}                                                             
      211 +                                                              
      212 +#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]                      
      213 +enum ExcursionState {                                         
      214 +    Depart,                                                   
      215 +    Away { until: f32 },                                      
      216 +    Return,                                                   
      217 +}                                                             
      218 +                                                              
      219 +/// Waddle off-screen, disappear for a while, and come back so
          +mewhere else — the                                            
      220 +/// "goose has other business" behavior (ADR 0016). Installed 
          +by the world as a timed                                       
      221 +/// interrupt over wandering; parameters (exit/entry points, a
          +way time) are chosen by                                       
      222 +/// the world's RNG so the whole thing stays deterministic per
          + seed.                                                        
      223 +pub struct ExcursionTask {                                    
      224 +    kind: ExcursionKind,                                      
      225 +    /// Off-screen point the goose walks out to.              
      226 +    exit: Vec2,                                               
      227 +    /// Off-screen point it reappears at after the away timer.
      228 +    entry: Vec2,                                              
      229 +    /// On-screen point it walks back in to.                  
      230 +    return_target: Vec2,                                      
      231 +    away_secs: f32,                                           
      232 +    state: ExcursionState,                                    
      233 +}                                                             
      234 +                                                              
      235 +impl ExcursionTask {                                          
      236 +    pub fn new(                                               
      237 +        kind: ExcursionKind,                                  
      238 +        exit: Vec2,                                           
      239 +        entry: Vec2,                                          
      240 +        return_target: Vec2,                                  
      241 +        away_secs: f32,                                       
      242 +    ) -> Self {                                               
      243 +        Self {                                                
      244 +            kind,                                             
      245 +            exit,                                             
      246 +            entry,                                            
      247 +            return_target,                                    
      248 +            away_secs,                                        
      249 +            state: ExcursionState::Depart,                    
      250 +        }                                                     
      251 +    }                                                         
      252 +}                                                             
      253 +                                                              
      254 +impl Task for ExcursionTask {                                 
      255 +    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {                            
      256 +        "excursion"                                           
      257 +    }                                                         
      258 +                                                              
      259 +    fn run(&mut self, goose: &mut GooseEntity, ctx: &mut TaskC
          +tx) -> bool {                                                 
      260 +        match self.state {                                    
      261 +            ExcursionState::Depart => {                       
      262 +                // A casual waddle out — deliberately NOT clam
          +ped to bounds.                                                
      263 +                goose.current_speed = goose.parameters.walk_sp
          +eed;                                                          
      264 +                goose.current_acceleration = goose.parameters.
          +acceleration_normal;                                          
      265 +                goose.target_pos = self.exit;                 
      266 +                if arrived(goose, 8.0) {                      
      267 +                    self.state = ExcursionState::Away {       
      268 +                        until: ctx.now + self.away_secs,      
      269 +                    };                                        
      270 +                }                                             
      271 +                false                                         
      272 +            }                                                 
      273 +            ExcursionState::Away { until } => {               
      274 +                // Parked out of sight; hold still (no footste
          +ps, no drift).                                                
      275 +                goose.target_pos = goose.position;            
      276 +                goose.velocity = Vec2::ZERO;                  
      277 +                if ctx.now >= until {                         
      278 +                    // Reappear at the staged entry point (sti
          +ll off-screen) and walk in.                                   
      279 +                    goose.position = self.entry;              
      280 +                    goose.target_pos = self.return_target;    
      281 +                    self.state = ExcursionState::Return;      
      282 +                }                                             
      283 +                false                                         
      284 +            }                                                 
      285 +            ExcursionState::Return => {                       
      286 +                goose.current_speed = goose.parameters.walk_sp
          +eed;                                                          
      287 +                goose.current_acceleration = goose.parameters.
          +acceleration_normal;                                          
      288 +                goose.target_pos = self.return_target;        
      289 +                if arrived(goose, 6.0) {                      
      290 +                    if let ExcursionKind::Puddle { mud_secs } 
          += self.kind {                                                 
      291 +                        // Came home through a puddle: track m
          +ud for a while.                                               
      292 +                        goose.track_mud_end_time = ctx.now + m
          +ud_secs;                                                      
      293 +                    }                                         
      294 +                    return true;                              
      295 +                }                                             
      296 +                false                                         
      297 +            }                                                 
      298 +        }                                                     
      299 +    }                                                         
      300 +}                                                             
      301 +                                                              
      302  /// The click→charge reaction: a short, fast, erratic "hyper" 
           burst (plan §5.6 hyper / M6).
      303  /// Installed as a transient interrupt when you click the goos
           e; when it finishes the world
      304  /// restores whatever task was running before. The full self-t
           riggered mood FSM is M13.

         +hly, it blinks, breathes when                                  
      32 +  idle, and flicks its tail when it honks. When it walks steepl
         +y up or down the screen you now                                
      33 +  see it from above instead of a sideways-rotated bird.        
      34 +- **Recolor everything.** Six goose colors (body, shading, wing
         +, beak, dark accents, outline) are                             
      35 +  now adjustable from the settings screen, and old saved settin
         +gs keep working.                                               
      36 +- **A goose with a life.** It wanders in curvy, gooselike paths
         + instead of straight lines; every                              
      37 +  few minutes it waddles off the edge of the screen, disappears
         + for a bit, and comes back —                                   
      38 +  sometimes with a prank in tow. Muddy footprints now have a st
         +ory: it only tracks mud after                                  
      39 +  nipping off-screen to (apparently) find a puddle, instead of 
         +being muddy all the time.                                      
      40 +- **More natural ways to say stop.** `goose exit` and `goose qu
         +it` now work just like                                         
      41 +  `goose stop`.                                                
      42 +- **Two more sliders in settings** for how close the goose must
         + get to grab the cursor and how                                
      43 +  far it drags it.                                             
      44 +                                                               
      45 +### Changed                                                    
      46 +- The goose's default colors are a touch softer and more design
         +ed; if you liked the old stark                                 
      47 +  white look you can dial it back in the color settings.

      82  ## Big-picture architecture (original → planned port)
      83  
      84  - **The goose is procedurally rendered, not a sprite** — there 
          is no sprite art anywhere.
      85 -  It's drawn each frame from a geometric rig (body/neck/head/ey
         -es/procedural feet) whose                                      
      86 -  constants live in `Exports.cs`. The port reimplements this re
         -nderer clean-room (no asset                                    
      87 -  extraction).                                                 
      85 +  It's drawn each frame from a geometric rig whose verified con
         +stants live in `Exports.cs`.                                   
      86 +  Renderer V2 (ADR 0014) draws it in the flat-illustration styl
         +e of the **project's own                                       
      87 +  reference art** (`docs/art-reference/`, Emmett's generated SV
         +Gs — design-time reference only;                               
      88 +  path geometry was transcribed into code and no image assets l
         +oad at runtime). The original                                  
      89 +  app's assets remain untouched (no extraction).               
      90  - **Engine = fixed 120 Hz tick + a Task state machine.** A defa
          ult "roaming" state picks
      91    random tasks via a shuffle-bag (`Deck`); a task only sets `ta
          rgetPos`/acceleration and the
      92    engine auto-locomotes toward it. Mod hooks fire Pre/Post Tick
           / UpdateRig / Render.

      293 +/// Render one goose view into a fresh transparent pixmap at a
          +n arbitrary vector scale                                      
      294 +/// (px per world px) with **no** downsampling — crisp output 
          +for tools/marketing/site                                      
      295 +/// assets. `origin` is the world point mapped to the pixmap's
          + top-left; the pixmap is                                      
      296 +/// `width`×`height` *world units* large before scaling.      
      297 +pub fn render_rig_scaled(                                     
      298 +    rig: &Rig,                                                
      299 +    origin: Vec2,                                             
      300 +    world_width: f32,                                         
      301 +    world_height: f32,                                        
      302 +    scale: f32,                                               
      303 +    palette: RenderPalette,                                   
      304 +) -> Option<Pixmap> {                                         
      305 +    let w = (world_width * scale).ceil() as u32;              
      306 +    let h = (world_height * scale).ceil() as u32;             
      307 +    let mut pixmap = Pixmap::new(w.max(1), h.max(1))?;        
      308 +    pixmap.fill(Color::TRANSPARENT);                          
      309 +    match rig.view {                                          
      310 +        RigView::Side { .. } => side::paint_side(&mut pixmap, 
          +rig, origin, scale, &palette),                                
      311 +        RigView::TopDown { .. } => top::paint_top(&mut pixmap,
          + rig, origin, scale, &palette),                               
      312 +    }                                                         
      313 +    Some(pixmap)                                              
      314 +}                                                             
      315 +

      261 +function detectOS() {                                         
      262 +  if (typeof navigator === "undefined") {                     
      263 +    return null;                                              
      264 +  }                                                           
      265 +  const uaData = navigator.userAgentData;                     
      266 +  const platform = (uaData?.platform || navigator.platform || 
          +"").toLowerCase();                                            
      267 +  const ua = (navigator.userAgent || "").toLowerCase();       
      268 +  const hay = `${platform} ${ua}`;                            
      269 +  if (/win/.test(hay)) {                                      
      270 +    return "windows";                                         
      271 +  }                                                           
      272 +  if (/mac|iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(hay)) {                     
      273 +    return "macos";                                           
      274 +  }                                                           
      275 +  if (/linux|x11|android|cros/.test(hay)) {                   
      276 +    return "linux";                                           
      277 +  }                                                           
      278 +  return null;                                                
      279 +}                                                             
      280 +

      390  
      391 +// Sprite-sheet frame driver for the real renderer walk cycle.
          + Sets an exact                                                
      392 +// discrete background-position per frame (no steps() off-by-o
          +ne), and freezes on                                           
      393 +// a mid-stride frame when the visitor prefers reduced motion.
      394 +function useWalkFrame(count, fps) {                           
      395 +  const [frame, setFrame] = useState(0);                      
      396 +  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();                   
      397 +                                                              
      398 +  useEffect(() => {                                           
      399 +    if (reducedMotion) {                                      
      400 +      setFrame(Math.floor(count / 2));                        
      401 +      return undefined;                                       
      402 +    }                                                         
      403 +                                                              
      404 +    let raf = 0;                                              
      405 +    let last = 0;                                             
      406 +    let acc = 0;                                              
      407 +    let index = 0;                                            
      408 +    const spf = 1000 / fps;                                   
      409 +                                                              
      410 +    const step = (t) => {                                     
      411 +      if (!last) {                                            
      412 +        last = t;                                             
      413 +      }                                                       
      414 +      acc += t - last;                                        
      415 +      last = t;                                               
      416 +      while (acc >= spf) {                                    
      417 +        acc -= spf;                                           
      418 +        index = (index + 1) % count;                          
      419 +        setFrame(index);                                      
      420 +      }                                                       
      421 +      raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);                      
      422 +    };                                                        
      423 +                                                              
      424 +    raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);                        
      425 +    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);                   
      426 +  }, [count, fps, reducedMotion]);                            
      427 +                                                              
      428 +  return frame;                                               
      429 +}                                                             
      430 +                                                              
      431 +// Lightweight magnetic pull for a single call-to-action. Poin
          +ter offset feeds a                                            
      432 +// spring; leaves reset to center. Disabled entirely under red
          +uced motion.                                                  
      433 +function useMagnetic(strength = 0.4) {                        
      434 +  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();                   
      435 +  const ref = useRef(null);                                   
      436 +  const x = useMotionValue(0);                                
      437 +  const y = useMotionValue(0);                                
      438 +  const springX = useSpring(x, { stiffness: 220, damping: 16, 
          +mass: 0.4 });                                                 
      439 +  const springY = useSpring(y, { stiffness: 220, damping: 16, 
          +mass: 0.4 });                                                 
      440 +                                                              
      441 +  const onMouseMove = useCallback(                            
      442 +    (event) => {                                              
      443 +      if (reducedMotion || !ref.current) {                    
      444 +        return;                                               
      445 +      }                                                       
      446 +      const rect = ref.current.getBoundingClientRect();       
      447 +      x.set((event.clientX - (rect.left + rect.width / 2)) * s
          +trength);                                                     
      448 +      y.set((event.clientY - (rect.top + rect.height / 2)) * s
          +trength);                                                     
      449 +    },                                                        
      450 +    [reducedMotion, strength, x, y],                          
      451 +  );                                                          
      452 +                                                              
      453 +  const onMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {                    
      454 +    x.set(0);                                                 
      455 +    y.set(0);                                                 
      456 +  }, [x, y]);                                                 
      457 +                                                              
      458 +  if (reducedMotion) {                                        
      459 +    return { ref, style: undefined, onMouseMove: undefined, on
          +MouseLeave: undefined };                                      
      460 +  }                                                           
      461 +                                                              
      462 +  return { ref, style: { x: springX, y: springY }, onMouseMove
          +, onMouseLeave };                                             
      463 +}

      850 +  const [shown, setShown] = useState(reducedMotion ? text : ""
          +);                                                            
      851  
      852 +  useEffect(() => {

      857 +                                                              
      858 +    let raf = 0;                                              
      859 +    let start = 0;                                            
      860 +    const perChar = 150;                                      
      861 +    const holdAfter = 2600;                                   
      862 +    const cycle = text.length * perChar + holdAfter;          
      863 +                                                              
      864 +    const tick = (t) => {                                     
      865 +      if (!start) {                                           
      866 +        start = t;                                            
      867 +      }                                                       
      868 +      const elapsed = (t - start) % cycle;                    
      869 +      const chars = Math.min(text.length, Math.floor(elapsed /
          + perChar));                                                   
      870 +      setShown(text.slice(0, chars));                         
      871 +      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);                      
      872 +    };                                                        
      873 +                                                              
      874 +    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);                        
      875 +    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);                   
      876 +  }, [text, reducedMotion]);                                  
      877 +                                                              
      878    return (
      879 +    <code className="terminal-input">                         
      880 +      <span>{shown}</span>

  ---Subject: honk300 v0.2.0 is live — new renderer, all-platform packaging, and
  thegoose.app

  Body:

  honk300 is fully shipped and deployed. Summary of everything that landed:

  What changed
  - Renderer rebuilt ("Procedural Vector V2"): flat-illustration dual-view goose
  (side + top-down with crossfade), stateful plant-and-swing feet (no more
  sliding), S-curve neck, 6-tone configurable palette, blink/breath/tail
  secondary motion.
  - Reliability pass (all platforms): Windows Per-Monitor-V2 DPI, non-blocking
  collect-window (no more sim freeze / IPC timeout / Notepad zombies), Unix
  flock singleton, macOS event-drain + safe present, honest Linux overlay
  failure.
  - Idle-life behaviors: meandering walks, off-screen puddle-hops that bring
  back mud (30–90s), timed off-screen errands with occasional prank returns.
  - macOS now packages: universal2 .app + DMG, install/uninstall/update,
  unsigned personal-use.
  - All stop synonyms (stop / exit / quit / no honk / bad) verified working.
  - Website rebuilt: real animated goose, OS-aware install, live macOS
  downloads, full CLI reference.

  Key decisions (ADRs 0014–0017)
  - Superseded the sprite-atlas renderer plan with procedural vector V2 (keeps
  live palette editing + resolution independence).
  - macOS ships unsigned personal-use for now (signing/notarization deferred).
  - Windows advertises per-user (corporate) MSI first, global second.

  Install
  - Site: https://thegoose.app
  - Release: https://github.com/RealEmmettS/goose/releases/tag/v0.2.0
  - Windows (recommended, no admin):
  honk300-x86_64-pc-windows-msvc-corporate.msi
  - Windows one-liner: irm .../honk300-installer.ps1 | iex
  - macOS: honk300-universal2.dmg (or the shell installer)
  - Linux: curl -LsSf .../honk300-installer.sh | sh

  ---

  Happy to adjust the wording, subject, or level of detail first — and if you'd
  rather I not email at all, that's fine too.

● Done — the neck refinement is merged, deployed, and live at

  forward-leaning neck.

  What I changed

  Looking at the reference model, the neck should be one continuous silhouette
  with a nearly-straight, slightly-forward back edge. Two specific things were
  off:

  - The backward curve → the lower neck's control point was bowing backward 
  toward the tail (neck_c1 = neck_base − fwd·len·0.16). I removed that bow; the
  spine is now a near-straight, gently forward-leaning curve like the reference.
  - The seam → the neck base sat right on top of the shoulder line, so its
  outline crossed the body edge. I buried the base deeper into the shoulder mass
  (and widened it slightly) so the body fill covers it — the neck now emerges
  cleanly from the shoulders with no border.

  Verified in the rest, walk, and reach poses (the rest pose in particular is a
  clean, natural goose now).

● The CLI is already fully interchangeable by construction:

  - Install creates all three names as aliases to the same binary (COMMAND_NAMES
  = ["honk300", "honk", "goose"] — src/install.rs:9), copied on Windows and
  symlinked on Linux/macOS.
  - Parsing (normalize_args) never looks at which name invoked it — clap ignores
  arg0, and the goose-speak rewriting (plz/exit/quit/bad/no honk) is
  name-agnostic. The only production reference to a specific name is an internal
  honk300 --version self-check in update.rs.

  So every command already works identically under all three names. Let me lock
  that in with a comprehensive test so it can never regress.

● Both changes are shipped and verified live.

  1. Terminal — goose / honk / honk300 fully interchangeable

  They already were, by construction, and I've now verified it and locked it in:
  - install links all three names to the same binary (COMMAND_NAMES = 
  ["honk300", "honk", "goose"]).
  - Argument parsing (normalize_args) never looks at which name launched it —
  clap ignores arg0, and the goose-speak rewriting (plz/exit/quit/bad/no honk)
  is name-agnostic. The only place a specific name appears in production is an
  internal honk300 --version self-check.
  - Added all_three_names_are_fully_interchangeable — it parses every command
  form (start/stop + all goose-speak synonyms, do <all six actions>,
  config/reload/status/setup, install/uninstall with flags, update, and every
  global flag) under all three names and asserts identical results. 13 CLI tests
  green; merged to main (b4dab32).

  So goose stop, honk stop, honk300 stop — and the same for every other command
  — are guaranteed identical, and the test will catch any future regression.

  2. Site usage section — consistent naming + natural order

  Fixed the confusing honk300↔goose switching and reordered it the way you
  actually use it. Live now at https://thegoose.app (confirmed on production):

  - Consistent honk300 across the whole table (no more name-switching). A header
  note makes it explicit: "honk300, honk, and goose are the same command — 
  every line below works with any of the three names." The goose-speak aliases
  live as "Also: goose plz / no honk / exit / quit" on the start and stop rows.
  - Natural order: Start & stop → Configure (config/reload/setup/status) →
  Install & autostart (install/update/uninstall) → Pokes (all six do <action>s)
  → Global flags.

  Neither change needed a new release — the CLI change is test-only (no behavior
  change, since the behavior was already correct), and the site is a redeploy.
  Everything's live and verified.
